do
	local RS = game:GetService("RunService")
	RS:UnbindFromRenderStep("FlameState")
	RS:UnbindFromRenderStep("FlameClientSync")
	RS:UnbindFromRenderStep("FlameHitFx")
	RS:UnbindFromRenderStep("FlameKnifeSync")
	local loops = { "FlameRageLoop", "FlameHitLoop", "FlameWorldLoop", "FlameVisLoop", "FlameSeatCleaner", "FlameMoveLoop", "FlameJumpLoop", "FlameKnifeLoop" }
	for _, name in next, loops do
		if getgenv()[name] then
			pcall(function() getgenv()[name]:Disconnect() end)
			getgenv()[name] = nil
		end
	end
	local OldWindow = getgenv().FlameWindow
	if OldWindow and OldWindow.ScootWindow then
		pcall(function() OldWindow.ScootWindow:Destroy() end)
	end
	getgenv().FlameWindow = nil
	local OldText = getgenv().FlameStateText
	local OldShadow = getgenv().FlameStateShadow
	if OldText then pcall(function() OldText:Remove() end) end
	if OldShadow then pcall(function() OldShadow:Remove() end) end
	getgenv().FlameStateText = nil
	getgenv().FlameStateShadow = nil
	local Old = getgenv().Library or _G.Library
	if type(Old) == "table" then
		if type(Old.Unload) == "function" then
			pcall(Old.Unload, Old)
		elseif type(Old.Exit) == "function" then
			pcall(Old.Exit, Old)
		end
	end
	getgenv().Library = nil
	_G.Library = nil
end

local Scoot = loadstring(game:HttpGet("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/andicry/test/refs/heads/main/Scoot%20Ui"))()
Scoot.MenuKeybind = tostring(Enum.KeyCode.Insert)
print("DBG m0 scoot ok")

local Library = {}
Library.__index = Library

local function Pick(Data, Key)
	local Value = Data[Key]
	if Value == nil then
		Value = Data[string.lower(Key)]
	end
	return Value
end

local function Step(Decimals)
	local Number = tonumber(Decimals)
	if not Number or Number <= 0 then
		return 1
	end
	if Number >= 1 and Number == math.floor(Number) then
		local Out = 1
		for _ = 1, Number do
			Out = Out / 10
		end
		return Out
	end
	return Number
end

local function TipFor(Data)
	local Tip = Pick(Data, "Tooltip")
	if type(Tip) == "string" then
		return { Name = tostring(Pick(Data, "Name") or "Info"), Description = Tip }
	elseif type(Tip) == "table" then
		return Tip
	end
	return nil
end

local function ChainVisible(GateObj)
	local Visible = true
	local Node = GateObj
	while Node do
		if not (Node.Scoot.Value and true) then
			Visible = false
		end
		Node = Node.Parent
	end
	return Visible
end

local function Apply(GateObj)
	if not GateObj then
		return
	end
	local Visible = ChainVisible(GateObj)
	for _, Kid in ipairs(GateObj.Kids) do
		pcall(function()
			Kid:SetVisibility(Visible)
		end)
	end
	for _, Child in ipairs(GateObj.Gates) do
		Apply(Child)
	end
end

local function Link(Node, Control)
	local GateObj = Node.Gate
	if GateObj then
		pcall(function()
			Control:SetVisibility(ChainVisible(GateObj))
		end)
		GateObj.Kids[#GateObj.Kids + 1] = Control
	end
	return Control
end

function Library:Toggle(Data)
	Data = Data or {}
	local UserCallback = Pick(Data, "Callback") or function() end
	local Holder
	local ScootToggle = self.Sect:Toggle({
		Name = Pick(Data, "Name"),
		Flag = Pick(Data, "Flag"),
		Default = Pick(Data, "Default") and true or false,
		ToolTip = TipFor(Data),
		Callback = function(Value)
			if Holder then
				if Value then
					Apply(Holder)
				else
					Apply(Holder)
				end
			end
			UserCallback(Value)
		end,
	})
	Holder = { Scoot = ScootToggle, Parent = self.Gate, Kids = {}, Gates = {} }
	if self.Gate then
		local ParentGate = self.Gate
		ParentGate.Gates[#ParentGate.Gates + 1] = Holder
		ParentGate.Kids[#ParentGate.Kids + 1] = ScootToggle
		pcall(function()
			ScootToggle:SetVisibility(ChainVisible(ParentGate))
		end)
	end
	return setmetatable({ Sect = self.Sect, Gate = Holder, Scoot = ScootToggle }, Library)
end

function Library:Popup(Data)
	return self
end

function Library:Keybind(Data)
	Data = Data or {}
	return self.Scoot:Keybind({
		Flag = Pick(Data, "Flag"),
		Default = Pick(Data, "Default"),
		Mode = Pick(Data, "Mode"),
		Callback = Pick(Data, "Callback") or function() end,
	})
end

function Library:Slider(Data)
	Data = Data or {}
	local ScootSlider = self.Sect:Slider({
		Name = Pick(Data, "Name"),
		Flag = Pick(Data, "Flag"),
		Min = Pick(Data, "Min") or 0,
		Max = Pick(Data, "Max") or 100,
		Default = Pick(Data, "Default") or 0,
		Decimals = Step(Pick(Data, "Decimals")),
		Suffix = Pick(Data, "Suffix") or "",
		ToolTip = TipFor(Data),
		Callback = Pick(Data, "Callback") or function() end,
	})
	return Link(self, ScootSlider)
end

function Library:Dropdown(Data)
	Data = Data or {}
	local ScootDropdown = self.Sect:Dropdown({
		Name = Pick(Data, "Name"),
		Flag = Pick(Data, "Flag"),
		Items = Pick(Data, "Items") or {},
		Default = Pick(Data, "Default"),
		Multi = Pick(Data, "Multi") and true or false,
		ToolTip = TipFor(Data),
		Callback = Pick(Data, "Callback") or function() end,
	})
	return Link(self, ScootDropdown)
end

function Library:Textbox(Data)
	Data = Data or {}
	local ScootTextbox = self.Sect:Textbox({
		Name = Pick(Data, "Name"),
		Flag = Pick(Data, "Flag"),
		Default = Pick(Data, "Default") or "",
		Numeric = Pick(Data, "Numeric") and true or false,
		Finished = Pick(Data, "Finished") and true or false,
		Placeholder = Pick(Data, "Placeholder") or "...",
		ToolTip = TipFor(Data),
		Callback = Pick(Data, "Callback") or function() end,
	})
	return Link(self, ScootTextbox)
end

function Library:Button(Data)
	Data = Data or {}
	local ScootButton = self.Sect:Button()
	ScootButton:Add(tostring(Pick(Data, "Name") or "Button"), Pick(Data, "Callback") or function() end)
	return Link(self, ScootButton)
end

function Library:Label(Data)
	local Name = Data
	if type(Data) == "table" then
		Name = Pick(Data, "Name")
	end
	local ScootLabel = self.Sect:Label(tostring(Name or ""))
	local Node = setmetatable({ Sect = self.Sect, Gate = self.Gate, Label = ScootLabel }, Library)
	if self.Gate then
		Link(self, ScootLabel)
	end
	return Node
end

function Library:SetText(Text)
	local Host = self.Label
	if type(Host) == "table" then
		pcall(function()
			Host:SetText(tostring(Text or ""))
		end)
	end
end

function Library:SetVisibility(Bool)
	local Host = self.Label
	if type(Host) == "table" then
		pcall(function()
			Host:SetVisibility(Bool and true or false)
		end)
	end
end

function Library:Colorpicker(Data)
	Data = Data or {}
	local Host = self.Label
	if type(Host) ~= "table" then
		Host = self.Scoot
	end
	if type(Host) ~= "table" then
		Host = self:Label("").Label
	end
	return Host:Colorpicker({
		Flag = Pick(Data, "Flag"),
		Default = Pick(Data, "Default"),
		Alpha = Pick(Data, "Alpha") or 0,
		Callback = Pick(Data, "Callback") or function() end,
	})
end

function Library:Window(Data)
	Data = Data or {}
	local ScootWindow = Scoot:Window({ Logo = tostring(Pick(Data, "Logo") or ""), FadeTime = 0.3 })
	local ScootWatermark = Scoot:Watermark(tostring(Pick(Data, "Name") or ""))
	local ScootKeybindList = Scoot:KeybindList()
	return setmetatable({
		Sect = nil,
		Gate = nil,
		ScootWindow = ScootWindow,
		ScootWatermark = ScootWatermark,
		ScootKeybindList = ScootKeybindList,
		Pages = {},
		Name = Pick(Data, "Name"),
	}, Library)
end

function Library:Page(Data)
	Data = Data or {}
	local ScootPage = self.ScootWindow:Page({
		Name = Pick(Data, "Name"),
		Columns = Pick(Data, "Columns") or 2,
		SubPages = Pick(Data, "SubPages") and true or false,
	})
	local Node = setmetatable({ Sect = nil, Gate = nil, ScootPage = ScootPage, Pages = {}, Name = Pick(Data, "Name") }, Library)
	self.Pages[#self.Pages + 1] = Node
	return Node
end

function Library:Section(Data)
	Data = Data or {}
	local ScootSection = self.ScootPage:Section({
		Name = Pick(Data, "Name"),
		Side = Pick(Data, "Side") or 1,
	})
	return setmetatable({ Sect = ScootSection, Gate = nil }, Library)
end

function Library:InitSettings()
	return Scoot:CreateSettingsPage(self.ScootWindow, self.ScootWatermark, self.ScootKeybindList)
end

function Library:Notification(Data)
	Data = Data or {}
	return Scoot:Notification(tostring(Pick(Data, "Name") or ""), tostring(Pick(Data, "Description") or ""), tonumber(Pick(Data, "Time")) or 5)
end

getgenv().LilacTargetHUDSource = [==[local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")

local LocalScript = {}
LocalScript.TargetPlayer = nil
LocalScript.AutoTarget = false
LocalScript.Dead = false
LocalScript.Connections = {}


local RoseLight = Color3.fromRGB(255, 122, 130)
local RoseMid   = Color3.fromRGB(206, 34, 52)
local RoseDeep  = Color3.fromRGB(126, 6, 26)
local GlowCol   = Color3.fromRGB(255, 96, 112)
local White     = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)

local UIFont = Font.fromEnum(Enum.Font.GothamBold)

pcall(function()
	local function RegisterFont(name, weight, style, asset)
		if not isfile(asset.Id) then
			writefile(asset.Id, asset.Font)
		end

		if isfile(name .. ".font") then
			delfile(name .. ".font")
		end

		writefile(name .. ".font", HttpService:JSONEncode({
			name = name,
			faces = { {
				name = "Normal",
				weight = weight,
				style = style,
				assetId = getcustomasset(asset.Id),
			} },
		}))

		return getcustomasset(name .. ".font")
	end

	local proggy = RegisterFont("ProggyTiny", 100, "Normal", {
		Id = "ProggyTiny.ttf",
		Font = game:HttpGet("https://github.com/i77lhm/storage/raw/refs/heads/main/fonts/ProggyTiny.ttf"),
	})

	UIFont = Font.new(proggy, Enum.FontWeight.Regular, Enum.FontStyle.Normal)
end)

local FX, FY = 170, 140
local GLOW_SPEC = {
	{ 88, 0.900 }, { 106, 0.925 }, { 126, 0.945 },
	{ 148, 0.958 }, { 172, 0.968 }, { 198, 0.978 },
}

local roseLayers = {
	{ count = 5, w = 34, h = 46, radius = 21, start = 0, tilt = 8, top = RoseDeep, bottom = RoseMid },
	{ count = 5, w = 27, h = 36, radius = 13, start = 36, tilt = -7, top = RoseMid, bottom = RoseLight },
	{ count = 4, w = 19, h = 26, radius = 7, start = 12, tilt = 10, top = RoseMid, bottom = RoseLight },
}


local function mk(class, props, parent)
	local o = Instance.new(class)
	for k, v in pairs(props) do o[k] = v end
	o.Parent = parent
	return o
end

local function corner(obj, radius)
	return mk("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(radius, 0) }, obj)
end

local function stroke(obj, colour, thickness, transparency)
	return mk("UIStroke", {
		Color = colour,
		Thickness = thickness or 1,
		Transparency = transparency or 0,
		LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Round,
	}, obj)
end

local function grad(obj, top, bottom, rotation)
	return mk("UIGradient", {
		Rotation = rotation or 90,
		Color = ColorSequence.new({
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, top),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, bottom),
		}),
	}, obj)
end

local function label(props, parent)
	props.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	props.BorderSizePixel = 0
	props.FontFace = UIFont
	props.TextColor3 = White
	props.TextSize = props.TextSize or 9
	props.TextXAlignment = props.TextXAlignment or Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

	local t = mk("TextLabel", props, parent)
	local s = stroke(t, Color3.new(0, 0, 0), 1, 0)
	s.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Contextual
	return t
end


local gui = mk("ScreenGui", {
	Name = "LilacTargetHUD",
	ResetOnSpawn = false,
	IgnoreGuiInset = true,
	DisplayOrder = 999999,
	ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
}, player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui"))

local root = mk("Frame", {
	Name = "Root",
	AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
	Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
	Size = UDim2.new(0, 340, 0, 280),
	BackgroundTransparency = 1,
	Visible = false,
}, gui)


local flower = mk("Frame", {
	AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
	Position = UDim2.new(0, FX, 0, FY),
	Size = UDim2.new(0, 140, 0, 140),
	BackgroundTransparency = 1,
}, root)

local glowLayers = {}

for _, spec in ipairs(GLOW_SPEC) do
	local g = mk("Frame", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, spec[1], 0, spec[1]),
		BackgroundColor3 = GlowCol,
		BackgroundTransparency = spec[2],
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
	}, flower)

	corner(g, 1)
	table.insert(glowLayers, { Frame = g, Base = spec[2] })
end

local waves = {}

for i = 1, 4 do
	local ring = mk("Frame", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 92, 0, 92),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		ZIndex = 2,
	}, flower)

	corner(ring, 1)
	table.insert(waves, {
		Frame = ring,
		Stroke = stroke(ring, GlowCol, 2, 0.5),
		Offset = (i - 1) / 4,
	})
end

local petals = {}

for _, layer in ipairs(roseLayers) do
	for i = 1, layer.count do
		local angle = layer.start + (i - 1) * 360 / layer.count
		local rad = math.rad(angle)
		local home = UDim2.new(0.5, math.sin(rad) * layer.radius, 0.5, -math.cos(rad) * layer.radius)
		local rot = angle + layer.tilt

		local frame = mk("Frame", {
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
			Position = home,
			Size = UDim2.new(0, layer.w, 0, layer.h),
			BackgroundColor3 = layer.bottom,
			Rotation = rot,
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			ZIndex = 3,
		}, flower)

		corner(frame, 1)
		grad(frame, layer.top, layer.bottom, 90)

		table.insert(petals, {
			Frame = frame,
			Home = home,
			Angle = rot,
			Alpha = 0.04,
			Gone = false,
			Tween = nil,
			Tweening = false,
		})
	end
end

local bud = mk("Frame", {
	AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
	Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
	Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16),
	BackgroundColor3 = RoseDeep,
	BorderSizePixel = 0,
	ZIndex = 6,
}, flower)

corner(bud, 1)
stroke(bud, RoseLight, 1, 0.3)

local budCore = mk("Frame", {
	AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
	Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
	Size = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 8),
	BackgroundColor3 = RoseLight,
	BorderSizePixel = 0,
	ZIndex = 7,
}, flower)

corner(budCore, 1)


local nameLabel = label({
	Position = UDim2.new(0, FX + 54, 0, FY - 40),
	Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 14),
	AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X,
	TextSize = 12,
	Text = "Target",
}, root)

local distLabel = label({
	Position = UDim2.new(0, FX + 54, 0, FY - 24),
	Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 12),
	AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X,
	Text = "0m",
}, root)

local toolLabel = label({
	Position = UDim2.new(0, FX + 54, 0, FY - 12),
	Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 12),
	AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X,
	Text = "none",
}, root)


local fx = mk("Frame", {
	Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
	BackgroundTransparency = 1,
	BorderSizePixel = 0,
}, root)

local eliminated = label({
	AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0),
	Position = UDim2.new(0, FX, 0, 224),
	Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 24),
	AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X,
	TextSize = 14,
	TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
	RichText = true,
	Text = "Eliminated",
	Visible = false,
}, fx)


local idleValue = Instance.new("NumberValue")
idleValue.Value = 1

local idle = 1
idleValue.Changed:Connect(function(value)
	idle = value
end)


local function spawnPetals(count, spread, life)
	for _ = 1, count do
		local angle = math.random() * math.pi * 2
		local dist = math.random(math.floor(spread * 0.4), spread)

		local p = mk("Frame", {
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
			Position = UDim2.new(0, FX, 0, FY),
			Size = UDim2.new(0, math.random(8, 15), 0, math.random(12, 20)),
			BackgroundColor3 = RoseMid,
			Rotation = math.random(0, 360),
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
		}, fx)

		corner(p, 1)
		grad(p, RoseLight, RoseDeep, 90)

		TweenService:Create(p, TweenInfo.new(life, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
			Position = UDim2.new(0, FX + math.cos(angle) * dist, 0, FY + math.sin(angle) * dist),
			Rotation = math.random(-360, 360),
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		}):Play()

		task.delay(life + 0.1, function() p:Destroy() end)
	end
end

local function shockwave(delay, size, thickness)
	task.spawn(function()
		task.wait(delay)

		local ring = mk("Frame", {
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
			Position = UDim2.new(0, FX, 0, FY),
			Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 26),
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
		}, fx)

		corner(ring, 1)
		local s = stroke(ring, White, thickness, 0.05)

		local info = TweenInfo.new(0.95, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
		TweenService:Create(ring, info, { Size = UDim2.new(0, size, 0, size) }):Play()
		TweenService:Create(s, info, { Transparency = 1 }):Play()

		task.delay(1.05, function() ring:Destroy() end)
	end)
end

local function dropPetal(p)
	if not p or not p.Frame then return end
	local frame = p.Frame
	p.Tweening = true

	p.Tween = TweenService:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.85, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
		Position = UDim2.new(p.Home.X.Scale, p.Home.X.Offset + math.random(-16, 16), p.Home.Y.Scale, p.Home.Y.Offset + 96),
		Rotation = p.Angle + math.random(-150, 150),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
	})
	if p.Tween then p.Tween:Play() end

	task.delay(0.9, function()
		p.Tweening = false

		if p.Gone then
			frame.Visible = false
		end
	end)

	spawnPetals(3, 60, 0.9)
end

local function restorePetal(p, delay)
	local frame = p.Frame

	task.delay(delay, function()
		if p.Gone then return end

		frame.Visible = true
		p.Tweening = true

		frame.Position = UDim2.new(p.Home.X.Scale, p.Home.X.Offset + math.random(-18, 18), p.Home.Y.Scale, p.Home.Y.Offset + 92)
		frame.Rotation = p.Angle + math.random(-130, 130)
		frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1

		p.Tween = TweenService:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.75, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
			Position = p.Home,
			Rotation = p.Angle,
			BackgroundTransparency = p.Alpha,
		})
		if p.Tween then
			p.Tween.Completed:Once(function()
				p.Tweening = false
			end)
			p.Tween:Play()
		else
			p.Tweening = false
		end
	end)
end

local function setPetals(amount)
	amount = math.clamp(amount, 0, #petals)

	local dropIndex, restoreIndex = 0, 0

	for i, p in ipairs(petals) do
		local gone = i <= amount

		if gone and not p.Gone then
			p.Gone = true
			dropIndex += 1

			local lag = (dropIndex - 1) * 0.03
			if lag > 0 then
				task.delay(lag, function()
					if p.Gone then dropPetal(p) end
				end)
			else
				dropPetal(p)
			end
		elseif not gone and p.Gone then
			p.Gone = false

			if p.Tween then
				p.Tween:Cancel()
				p.Tween = nil
			end

			restoreIndex += 1
			restorePetal(p, (restoreIndex - 1) * 0.04)
		end
	end

	return restoreIndex
end

local function resetFlower()
	local missing = 0

	for _, p in ipairs(petals) do
		if p.Gone then missing += 1 end
	end

	setPetals(0)

	eliminated.Visible = false

	for _, child in ipairs(fx:GetChildren()) do
		if child ~= eliminated then child:Destroy() end
	end

	if missing > 0 then
		task.delay(0.75 + missing * 0.04, function()
			if not LocalScript.Dead then
				TweenService:Create(idleValue, TweenInfo.new(0.8, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Value = 1 }):Play()
			end
		end)
	else
		TweenService:Create(idleValue, TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Value = 1 }):Play()
	end
end

local function playKill(targetName)
	TweenService:Create(idleValue, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Value = 0 }):Play()

	for _, p in ipairs(petals) do
		p.Gone = true
		p.Tweening = true

		local frame = p.Frame
		frame.Visible = true

		if p.Tween then
			p.Tween:Cancel()
		end

		local angle = math.random() * math.pi * 2
		local dist = math.random(120, 280)

		p.Tween = TweenService:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.55, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
			Position = UDim2.new(p.Home.X.Scale, p.Home.X.Offset + math.cos(angle) * dist, p.Home.Y.Scale, p.Home.Y.Offset + math.sin(angle) * dist),
			Rotation = p.Angle + math.random(-220, 220),
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		})
		p.Tween:Play()
	end

	task.delay(0.7, function()
		for _, p in ipairs(petals) do
			p.Tweening = false
			if p.Gone then p.Frame.Visible = false end
		end
	end)

	local flash = mk("Frame", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, FX, 0, FY),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 40),
		BackgroundColor3 = White,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.25,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
	}, fx)

	corner(flash, 1)

	TweenService:Create(flash, TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 230, 0, 230),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
	}):Play()

	task.delay(0.6, function() flash:Destroy() end)

	shockwave(0, 300, 3)
	shockwave(0.11, 420, 2)
	shockwave(0.24, 560, 2)
	shockwave(0.40, 700, 1)

	spawnPetals(22, 300, 1.2)

	eliminated.Text = 'Eliminated <font color="#FF3B3B">' .. targetName .. '</font>'
	eliminated.TextTransparency = 1
	eliminated.Position = UDim2.new(0, FX, 0, 232)
	eliminated.Visible = true

	TweenService:Create(eliminated, TweenInfo.new(0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
		TextTransparency = 0,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, FX, 0, 224),
	}):Play()

	task.delay(1.6, function()
		if eliminated.Visible then
			TweenService:Create(eliminated, TweenInfo.new(0.5), { TextTransparency = 1 }):Play()
			task.wait(0.55)
			eliminated.Visible = false
		end
	end)
end


function LocalScript:SetTarget(plr)
	self.AutoTarget = false

	if plr ~= self.TargetPlayer then
		resetFlower()
		self.TargetPlayer = plr
		self.Dead = false

		nameLabel.Text = plr and plr.DisplayName or "Target"
		if not plr then
			distLabel.Text = "0m"
			toolLabel.Text = "none"
		end
	end
end

function LocalScript:ClearTarget()
	resetFlower()
	self.TargetPlayer = nil
	self.Dead = false
	eliminated.Visible = false
	root.Visible = false
end

function LocalScript:Unload()
	self.Unloaded = true
	self.AutoTarget = false
	self:ClearTarget()
	for _, C in pairs(self.Connections or {}) do
		pcall(function()
			C:Disconnect()
		end)
	end
	pcall(function()
		gui:Destroy()
	end)
end


local function pickTarget()
	local camPos = Camera.CFrame.Position
	local camLook = Camera.CFrame.LookVector
	local best, bestScore = nil, 0.88

	for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if plr ~= player then
			local char = plr.Character
			local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			local part = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if hum and part and hum.Health > 0 then
				local offset = part.Position - camPos
				local dist = offset.Magnitude

				if dist > 1 and dist < 300 then
					local dot = camLook:Dot(offset / dist)
					if dot > bestScore then
						bestScore = dot
						best = plr
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return best
end


local thresholds = {}

for i = 1, #petals do
	table.insert(thresholds, 0.93 - (i - 1) * (0.86 / math.max(#petals - 1, 1)))
end

local gone = 0
local lastPct = -1

task.spawn(function()
	while true do
		if LocalScript.Unloaded then
			break
		end
		if LocalScript.AutoTarget and not LocalScript.Dead then
			local found = pickTarget()

			if found ~= LocalScript.TargetPlayer then
				resetFlower()
				LocalScript.TargetPlayer = found
				nameLabel.Text = found and found.DisplayName or "Target"
				distLabel.Text = "0m"
				toolLabel.Text = "none"
				lastPct = -1
			end
		end

		task.wait(0.15)
	end
end)


local animT = 0
local healthPct = 1

LocalScript.Connections.Render = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
	animT += dt

	flower.Rotation = math.sin(animT * 1.1) * 4 * idle

	for i, layer in ipairs(glowLayers) do
		layer.Frame.BackgroundTransparency = math.clamp(
			layer.Base + (1 - healthPct) * 0.10 + math.sin(animT * 1.7 + i * 0.8) * 0.018 * idle, 0, 0.98)
	end

	for _, w in ipairs(waves) do
		local p = (animT * 0.38 + w.Offset) % 1
		local size = 92 + p * 190
		w.Frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, size, 0, size)
		w.Stroke.Transparency = 1 - idle * (1 - (0.28 + p * 0.72))
	end

	for i, p in ipairs(petals) do
		if not p.Tweening then
			p.Frame.Rotation = p.Angle + math.sin(animT * 1.5 + i * 0.6) * 2.5 * idle
		end
	end

	local pulse = 15 + math.sin(animT * 2.6) * 1.6 * idle
	bud.Size = UDim2.new(0, pulse, 0, pulse)

	local target = LocalScript.TargetPlayer
	if not target or not target.Parent then
		root.Visible = false
		return
	end

	local char = target.Character
	local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	local part = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

	if not hum or not part then
		root.Visible = false
		return
	end

	root.Visible = true

	distLabel.Text = string.format("%dm", math.floor((Camera.CFrame.Position - part.Position).Magnitude + 0.5))

	local tool = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
	toolLabel.Text = tool and tool.Name or "none"

	local pct = math.clamp(math.max(hum.Health, 0) / math.max(hum.MaxHealth, 1), 0, 1)
	healthPct = pct

	if math.abs(pct - lastPct) > 0.004 then
		lastPct = pct

		local amount = 0
		for i = 1, #thresholds do
			if pct <= thresholds[i] then amount = i end
		end

		if amount ~= gone then
			setPetals(amount)
			gone = amount
		end
	end

	local KnockedNow = false
	local TargetEffects = char:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
	local KnockedFlag = TargetEffects and TargetEffects:FindFirstChild("K.O")
	if KnockedFlag then
		KnockedNow = KnockedFlag.Value == true
	end

	if (pct <= 0 or KnockedNow) and not LocalScript.Dead then
		LocalScript.Dead = true
		playKill(nameLabel.Text)

		task.delay(2.6, function()
			if LocalScript.Dead then
				LocalScript.TargetPlayer = nil
				LocalScript.Dead = false
				root.Visible = false
				resetFlower()
				gone = 0
				lastPct = -1
			end
		end)
	end
end)


if getgenv then
	getgenv().LilacTargetHUD = LocalScript
end

return LocalScript

	pcall(function()

	end)

getgenv().FlameGunHandler = nil

	getgenv().FlameGunHandler = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Modules"):WaitForChild("GunHandler"))
end)

if type(getgenv().FlameGunHandler) == "table" and type(getgenv().FlameGunHandler.getAim) == "function" then
	if not getgenv().FlameGetAimOriginal then
		getgenv().FlameGetAimOriginal = getgenv().FlameGunHandler.getAim
	end
	getgenv().FlameGunHandler.getAim = function(HitPos, Range)
		if Config.TargetAim then
			local Caller = nil
			pcall(function()
				Caller = getcallingscript and getcallingscript()
			end)
			if Caller == nil or (Caller.Name and Caller.Name:match("Client")) then
				local AimTarget = Config.TargetAimCurrent or Config.BotTarget
				local AimCharacter = AimTarget and AimTarget.Character
				local AimPart = AimCharacter and (AimCharacter:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or AimCharacter:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso") or AimCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
				if AimPart then
					local Direction = AimPart.Position - HitPos
					return Direction, Direction.Magnitude
				end
			end
		end
		local Original = getgenv().FlameGetAimOriginal
		if Original then
			return Original(HitPos, Range)
		end
	end
end

getgenv().FlameTargetAimTracer = nil
pcall(function()
	local Line = Drawing.new("Line")
	Line.Visible = false
	Line.Thickness = Config.TargetAimTracerThickness
	Line.Transparency = 0
	Line.Color = Config.TargetAimTracerColor
	getgenv().FlameTargetAimTracer = Line
end)

RunService:BindToRenderStep("FlameTargetAimTracer", 98, function()
	local Tracer = getgenv().FlameTargetAimTracer
	if not Tracer then
		return
	end
	if not Config.TargetAim or not Config.TargetAimTracer then
		if Tracer.Visible then
			Tracer.Visible = false
		end
		return
	end

	local AimTarget = Config.BotTarget
	local AimCharacter = AimTarget and AimTarget.Character
	local AimPart = AimCharacter and (AimCharacter:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or AimCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
	local MyCharacter = LocalPlayer.Character
	local MyTool = MyCharacter and MyCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
	local MyHandle = MyTool and MyTool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
	local MyRoot = MyCharacter and MyCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local Origin = MyHandle and MyHandle.Position or (MyRoot and MyRoot.Position)

	if not AimPart or not Origin then
		Tracer.Visible = false
		return
	end

	local From, FromOn = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(Origin)
	local To, ToOn = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(AimPart.Position)
	if not FromOn or not ToOn then
		Tracer.Visible = false
		return
	end

	Tracer.From = Vector2.new(From.X, From.Y)
	Tracer.To = Vector2.new(To.X, To.Y)
	Tracer.Thickness = Config.TargetAimTracerThickness
	Tracer.Color = Config.TargetAimTracerColor
	Tracer.Visible = true
end)
getgenv().FlameTargetAimTracerLoop = true

if not getgenv().FlameGunModsHooked then
	getgenv().FlameGunModsHooked = true
	pcall(function()
		local GunHandler = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Modules"):WaitForChild("GunHandler"))
		if type(GunHandler) == "table" and type(GunHandler.getCanShoot) == "function" then
			GunHandler.getCanShoot = function(Tool)
				if Config.RapidFire then
					return true
				end
				local Item = Tool and Tool:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
				if Item then
					local Ammo = Item:FindFirstChild("Ammo")
					return Ammo and Ammo.Value > 0
				end
				return true
			end
		end
	end)
	pcall(function()
		local OriginalTick = tick
		local _ = (function()
			if Config.RapidFire then
				local Trace = debug.traceback()
				if Trace and Trace:match("Gun") then
					if Config.RapidFireAmount >= 100 or math.random(1, 100) <= Config.RapidFireAmount then
						return math.huge
					end
				end
			end
			return OriginalTick()
		end)
	end)
	pcall(function()
		local OriginalRandom = math.random
		local _ = (function(A, B)
			if Config.NoSpread and checkcaller and not checkcaller() then
				if A == nil and B == nil then
					return OriginalRandom() * (Config.NoSpreadAmount / 100)
				elseif A == -0.05 and B == 0.05 then
					return OriginalRandom(A, B) * (Config.NoSpreadAmount / 100)
				elseif A == -0.1 or A == -0.05 then
					return OriginalRandom(A, B) * (Config.NoSpreadAmount / 100)
				end
			end
			return OriginalRandom(A, B)
		end)
	end)
end

if not getgenv().FlameNewIndexHooked then
	getgenv().FlameNewIndexHooked = true
	pcall(function()
		local OldNewIndex
		OldNewIndex = hookmetamethod(game, "__newindex", function(Self, Key, Value)
			if checkcaller and not checkcaller() and (Config.NoJumpCooldown or Config.NoSlow) and type(Self) == "userdata" and Self:IsA("Humanoid") then
				if Config.NoJumpCooldown and Key == "JumpPower" then
					return
				end
				if Config.NoSlow and Key == "WalkSpeed" then
					local Number = tonumber(Value)
					if Number and Number < 16 then
						Value = 16
					end
				end
			end
			return OldNewIndex(Self, Key, Value)
		end)
	end)
end

getgenv().FlameTargetAimFOVCircle = nil
getgenv().FlameTargetAimFOVOutline = nil

getgenv().FlameTargetAimPickPlayer = function(UseMouse)
	local MousePos = nil
	pcall(function()
		MousePos = game:GetService("UserInputService"):GetMouseLocation()
	end)
	local MyCharacter = LocalPlayer.Character
	local MyRoot = MyCharacter and MyCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local Best, BestScore = nil, nil

	for _, Player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if Player ~= LocalPlayer then
			local Character = Player.Character
			local Root = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local Humanoid = Character and Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

			if Root and Humanoid then
				local Effects = Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
				local KnockedFlag = Effects and Effects:FindFirstChild("K.O")
				local DeadFlag = Effects and Effects:FindFirstChild("Dead")
				local IsKnockedNow = KnockedFlag and KnockedFlag.Value == true
				local IsDeadNow = DeadFlag and DeadFlag.Value == true or Humanoid.Health <= 0

				if not IsDeadNow then
					if Config.TargetAimIgnoreKnocked and IsKnockedNow then
						continue
					end
					if Config.TargetAimIgnoreInvulnerable and (Character:FindFirstChild("ForceField") or Character:FindFirstChild("FORCEFIELD")) then
						continue
					end
					if Config.TargetAimIgnoreVoid and Root.Position.Magnitude > 9e8 then
						continue
					end
					if Config.TargetAimIgnoreFriend then
						local Ok, IsFriend = pcall(function()
							return LocalPlayer:IsFriendsWith(Player.UserId)
						end)
						if Ok and IsFriend then
							continue
						end
					end
					if Config.TargetAimIgnoreWall and MyRoot then
						local Params = RaycastParams.new()
						Params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
						Params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { MyCharacter, Character }
						if Workspace:Raycast(MyRoot.Position, Root.Position - MyRoot.Position, Params) then
							continue
						end
					end

					local Score
					if UseMouse and MousePos then
						local Screen, OnScreen = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(Root.Position)
						if not OnScreen then
							continue
						end
						Score = (Vector2.new(Screen.X, Screen.Y) - MousePos).Magnitude

						if Config.TargetAimFOV < 180 then
							local Radius = (Camera.ViewportSize.Magnitude / (math.pi / 2)) * (Config.TargetAimFOV / 180)
							if Score > Radius then
								continue
							end
						end
					elseif MyRoot then
						Score = (Root.Position - MyRoot.Position).Magnitude
					else
						Score = 0
					end

					if Config.TargetAimPrioritize == "lowest health" then
						Score = Humanoid.Health
					end

					if BestScore == nil or Score < BestScore then
						BestScore = Score
						Best = Player
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return Best
end

getgenv().FlameTargetAimPickCurrent = function()
	return getgenv().FlameTargetAimPickPlayer(Config.TargetAimPrioritize == "closest to mouse")
end

getgenv().FlameDoStomp = function(VictimCharacter)
	local Root = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not Root then
		return
	end
	local TorsoPart = VictimCharacter:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or VictimCharacter:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso") or VictimCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not TorsoPart then
		return
	end
	for _, Part in ipairs(LocalPlayer.Character:GetDescendants()) do
		if Part:IsA("BasePart") and Part.CanCollide then
			pcall(function()
				Part.CanCollide = false
			end)
		end
	end
	pcall(function()
		sethiddenproperty(Root, "PhysicsRepRootRef", InstanceHandle.new(nil))
	end)
	ServerCFrame = CFrame.new(TorsoPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, Config.StompOffset, 0))
	ServerVelocity = Vector3.zero
	if os.clock() - LastStomp > StompDelay then
		LastStomp = os.clock()
		pcall(function()
			MainEvent:FireServer("Stomp")
		end)
	end
end

local NoClipConnection = nil

local NoClipSetup = nil

local function ApplyNoClip(Character)
	if not Character then
		return
	end
	if NoClipSetup ~= Character then
		NoClipSetup = Character
		if NoClipConnection then
			NoClipConnection:Disconnect()
			NoClipConnection = nil
		end
			NoClipConnection = Character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(Desc)
			if Desc:IsA("BasePart") then
				Desc.CanCollide = false
			end
		end)
	end
	for _, Part in ipairs(Character:GetDescendants()) do
		if Part:IsA("BasePart") and Part.CanCollide then
			Part.CanCollide = false
		end
	end
end

local AntiSitSetup = nil

local function ApplyAntiSit(Character)
	if not Character or AntiSitSetup == Character then
		return
	end
	AntiSitSetup = Character
	local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if Humanoid then
		pcall(function()
			Humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated, false)
		end)
	end
end

local function UnseatSelf(Character)
	local Humanoid = Character and Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not Humanoid or not Humanoid.Sit then
		return
	end
	local SeatPart = Humanoid.SeatPart
	pcall(function()
		Humanoid.Sit = false
		Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
	end)
	local Root = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if Root then
		for _, Joint in ipairs(Root:GetJoints()) do
			if Joint:IsA("Weld") or Joint:IsA("WeldConstraint") then
				local P0 = Joint.Part0
				local P1 = Joint.Part1
				local Other = nil
				if P0 and P0:IsDescendantOf(Character) and P1 and not P1:IsDescendantOf(Character) then
					Other = P1
				elseif P1 and P1:IsDescendantOf(Character) and P0 and not P0:IsDescendantOf(Character) then
					Other = P0
				end
				if Other then
					local Parent = Other:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
					if Parent and Parent.Name == "Vehicles" or (Parent and Parent.Parent and Parent.Parent.Name == "Vehicles") then
						pcall(function()
							Joint:Destroy()
						end)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	if SeatPart and (SeatPart:IsA("Seat") or SeatPart:IsA("VehicleSeat")) then
		pcall(function()
			SeatPart.Occupant = nil
			Humanoid.SeatPart = nil
			SeatPart:Destroy()
		end)
	end
end

local SeatConnection = nil

local function KillSeat(Desc)
	if Config.AntiSit and (Desc:IsA("Seat") or Desc:IsA("VehicleSeat")) then
		pcall(function()
			Desc:Destroy()
		end)
	end
end

local function StartSeatCleaner()
	if SeatConnection then
		return
	end
	if getgenv().FlameSeatCleaner then
		pcall(function()
			getgenv().FlameSeatCleaner:Disconnect()
		end)
	end
	for _, Desc in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
		KillSeat(Desc)
	end
	SeatConnection = Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(KillSeat)
	getgenv().FlameSeatCleaner = SeatConnection
end

local function GetRoot()
	local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
	if Character then
		return Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	end
end

local function PlayerTag(Player)
	if not Player then
		return "?"
	end
	return tostring(Player.DisplayName or Player.Name) .. "/" .. Player.Name
end

local function PlayerFromValue(Value)
	if not Value then
		return nil
	end
	local Kind = typeof(Value)
	if Kind == "Instance" then
		if Value:IsA("Player") then
			return Value
		end
		if Value:IsA("Model") then
			local Player = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Value)
			if Player then
				return Player
			end
		end
		return Players:FindFirstChild(Value.Name)
	end
	if Kind == "string" then
		return Players:FindFirstChild(Value)
	end
	return nil
end

local function GetFlamethrower()
	local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
	if Character then
		local Tool = Character:FindFirstChild("[Flamethrower]")
		if Tool then
			return Tool
		end
	end
	local Backpack = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
	if Backpack then
		return Backpack:FindFirstChild("[Flamethrower]")
	end
end

local function OwnsFlamethrower()
	return GetFlamethrower() ~= nil
end

local FoodNames = {
	"pizza",
	"chicken",
	"hamburger",
	"taco",
	"hotdog",
	"cranberry",
	"lemonade",
	"lettuce",
	"meat",
	"donut",
	"popcorn",
	"popsicle",
	"icecream",
	"starblox latte",
	"da milk",
}

local FoodSet = {}
for _, Name in ipairs(FoodNames) do
	FoodSet[Name] = true
end

local function IsFoodTool(Tool)
	if not Tool or not Tool:IsA("Tool") then
		return false
	end
	local Name = string.lower(Tool.Name)
	Name = string.gsub(Name, "^%[", "")
	Name = string.gsub(Name, "%]$", "")
	Name = string.gsub(Name, "^%s*(.-)%s*$", "%1")
	return FoodSet[Name] == true
end

local function GetFood()
	local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
	if Character then
		for _, Child in ipairs(Character:GetChildren()) do
			if IsFoodTool(Child) then
				return Child
			end
		end
	end
	local Backpack = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
	if Backpack then
		for _, Child in ipairs(Backpack:GetChildren()) do
			if IsFoodTool(Child) then
				return Child
			end
		end
	end
end

local function CountFood()
	local Count = 0
	local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
	if Character then
		for _, Child in ipairs(Character:GetChildren()) do
			if IsFoodTool(Child) then
				Count = Count + 1
			end
		end
	end
	local Backpack = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
	if Backpack then
		for _, Child in ipairs(Backpack:GetChildren()) do
			if IsFoodTool(Child) then
				Count = Count + 1
			end
		end
	end
	return Count
end

local function EatFood()
	local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
	if not Character then
		return
	end
	local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not Humanoid then
		return
	end
	local Tool = GetFood()
	if not Tool then
		return
	end
	if Tool.Parent ~= Character then
		pcall(function()
			Humanoid:EquipTool(Tool)
		end)
	end
	pcall(function()
		Tool:Activate()
	end)
end

local function SetCrouch(On)
	pcall(function()
		game:GetService("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(On, Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl, false, game)
	end)
end

local function EquipFlamethrower()
	local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
	if not Character then
		return nil
	end
	local Tool = Character:FindFirstChild("[Flamethrower]")
	if Tool then
		return Tool
	end
	local Backpack = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
	local Stored = Backpack and Backpack:FindFirstChild("[Flamethrower]")
	if not Stored then
		return nil
	end
	local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if Humanoid then
		pcall(function()
			Humanoid:EquipTool(Stored)
		end)
	end
	if Stored.Parent ~= Character then
		pcall(function()
			Stored.Parent = Character
		end)
	end
	return Stored
end

local function Glue(Part)
	local Root = GetRoot()
	if Root then
		pcall(function()
			sethiddenproperty(Root, "PhysicsRepRootRef", InstanceHandle.new(Part))
		end)
	end
end

local function Unglue()
	local Root = GetRoot()
	if Root then
		pcall(function()
			sethiddenproperty(Root, "PhysicsRepRootRef", InstanceHandle.new(nil))
		end)
	end
end

local function StrafePosition(AimPosition)
	local Distance = Config.FollowDistance
	local Radius = Distance
	local Angle
	local Now = os.clock()
	if Config.FollowStyle == "random" then
		Angle = math.random() * math.pi * 2
		LastStrafeAngle = Angle
		LastStrafeRadius = Distance
		LastStrafeHeight = 0
	else
		if not LastStrafeAngle or Now - LastStrafeTick > 0.025 then
			LastStrafeTick = Now
			LastStrafeAngle = math.random() * math.pi * 2
			LastStrafeHeight = math.random(-3, 3)
			LastStrafeRadius = Distance * (0.5 + math.random() * 1.1)
		end
		Angle = LastStrafeAngle
		Radius = LastStrafeRadius
	end
	local Height = LastStrafeHeight + math.sin(Now * 9) * 1.5
	return AimPosition + Vector3.new(math.sin(Angle) * Radius, Height, math.cos(Angle) * Radius)
end


local VoidFallHeight = nil
pcall(function()
	VoidFallHeight = workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight
end)

local function EnsureVoidSurvivable()
	local Height = workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight
	if Height == Height then
		pcall(function()
			workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = 0 / 0
		end)
	end
end

local function RestoreVoidSurvivable()
	if VoidFallHeight == nil then
		return
	end
	pcall(function()
		workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = VoidFallHeight
	end)
end

local function VoidFarAxis()
	return math.random(1000000, 2147483647) * (math.random(0, 1) == 0 and -1 or 1)
end

local function VoidUnderPos()
	return Vector3.new(VoidFarAxis(), math.random(-350, -150), VoidFarAxis())
end

local function VoidCFrame()
	return CFrame.new(VoidUnderPos())
end

local VoidEdgeOffsets = {
	Vector3.new(-1414, 0, -1414),
	Vector3.new(1414, 0, -1414),
	Vector3.new(-1414, 0, 1414),
	Vector3.new(1414, 0, 1414),
	Vector3.new(1414, 1414, 1414),
	Vector3.new(-1414, 1414, 1414),
	Vector3.new(1414, 1414, -1414),
}
print("DBG m1 before void edge")
local VoidEdgePoint = nil
local VoidEdgeSwitch = 0
local VoidDriftPos = nil
local VoidDriftVel = Vector3.new(math.random(-90000, 90000), 0, math.random(-90000, 90000))
print("DBG m2 after drift vel")
local VoidShuffleSign = Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)
local VoidPingA = nil
local VoidPingB = nil
local VoidPingSwitch = 0
local VoidPingFlip = false
local VoidFreezePoint = nil
local VoidParkPoint = nil
local VoidParkSwitch = 0

local function GetVoidPoint()
	local Method = Config.VoidMethod
	if Method == "Map Edges" then
		local Now = os.clock()
		if not VoidEdgePoint or Now - VoidEdgeSwitch > 0.05 then
			VoidEdgeSwitch = Now
			VoidEdgePoint = VoidEdgeOffsets[math.random(1, #VoidEdgeOffsets)] * 1500
		end
		return VoidEdgePoint
	elseif Method == "Straight Up" then
		return Vector3.new(0, math.random(1000000, 2147483647), 0)
	elseif Method == "Far Away" then
		return Vector3.new(9e15, 9e15, 9e15)
	elseif Method == "Shuffle" then
		VoidShuffleSign = Vector3.new(-VoidShuffleSign.X, 0, -VoidShuffleSign.Z)
		local Far = 2000000000
		return Vector3.new(VoidShuffleSign.X * math.random(Far - 40000000, Far), math.random(-350, -150), VoidShuffleSign.Z * math.random(Far - 40000000, Far))
	elseif Method == "Ping Pong" then
		local Now = os.clock()
		if not VoidPingA or Now - VoidPingSwitch > 0.25 then
			VoidPingSwitch = Now
			VoidPingA = VoidUnderPos()
			VoidPingB = Vector3.new(-VoidPingA.X, math.random(-350, -150), -VoidPingA.Z)
		end
		VoidPingFlip = not VoidPingFlip
		if VoidPingFlip then
			return VoidPingA
		end
		return VoidPingB
	elseif Method == "Freeze" then
		if not VoidFreezePoint then
			VoidFreezePoint = VoidUnderPos()
		end
		return VoidFreezePoint
	elseif Method == "Drift" then
		VoidDriftPos = (VoidDriftPos or VoidUnderPos()) + VoidDriftVel
		local Flat = Vector3.new(VoidDriftPos.X, 0, VoidDriftPos.Z).Magnitude
		if Flat > 2000000000 or Flat < 1000000 then
			VoidDriftPos = VoidUnderPos()
			VoidDriftVel = Vector3.new(math.random(-90000, 90000), 0, math.random(-90000, 90000))
		end
		return VoidDriftPos
	elseif Method == "Under Map" then
		local Now = os.clock()
		if not VoidParkPoint or Now - VoidParkSwitch > 2 then
			VoidParkSwitch = Now
			VoidParkPoint = VoidUnderPos()
		end
		return VoidParkPoint
	end
	return VoidUnderPos()
end

local function StartVoid(Root)
	if HoldMode ~= "void" then
		VoidReturn = Root.CFrame
	end
	HoldMode = "void"
	if not VoidPoint then
		VoidPoint = VoidCFrame().Position
	end
end

local function HoldVoid(Root)
	EnsureVoidSurvivable()
	pcall(function()
		sethiddenproperty(Root, "PhysicsRepRootRef", InstanceHandle.new(nil))
	end)
	VoidPoint = GetVoidPoint()
	ServerCFrame = CFrame.new(VoidPoint)
	ServerVelocity = VoidPush
end

local function ReleaseVoid()
	local Root = GetRoot()
	if HoldMode == "void" and VoidReturn and Root and Root.Parent then
		ServerCFrame = VoidReturn
		ServerVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -0.1, 0)
	end
	HoldMode = nil
end

local HealVoidPoint = nil
local HealVoidSwitch = 0

local function HoldHealVoid(Root)
	EnsureVoidSurvivable()
	pcall(function()
		sethiddenproperty(Root, "PhysicsRepRootRef", InstanceHandle.new(nil))
	end)
	local Now = os.clock()
	if not HealVoidPoint or Now - HealVoidSwitch > 2 then
		HealVoidSwitch = Now
		local Far = math.random(1000000000, 2147483647)
		HealVoidPoint = Vector3.new(math.random(0, 1) == 0 and -Far or Far, -2147483647, math.random(0, 1) == 0 and -Far or Far)
	end
	ServerCFrame = CFrame.new(HealVoidPoint)
	ServerVelocity = VoidPush
end

local function GetFlag(Effects, Name)
	local Value = Effects and Effects:FindFirstChild(Name)
	if Value then
		return Value.Value
	end
end

local function GetFlag(Effects, Name)
	local Value = Effects and Effects:FindFirstChild(Name)
	if Value then
		return Value.Value
	end
end

local function IsDead(Character)
	if not Character then
		return false
	end
	local Effects = Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
	if Effects then
		local SDeath = Effects:FindFirstChild("SDeath")
		if SDeath and SDeath.Value == true then
			return true
		end
		local Dead = Effects:FindFirstChild("Dead")
		if Dead and Dead.Value == true then
			return true
		end
	end
	local Head = Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
	if Head == nil then
		return true
	end
	return false
end

local function DeadFlag(Character)
	if not Character then
		return false
	end
	local Effects = Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
	if not Effects then
		return false
	end
	local Dead = Effects:FindFirstChild("Dead")
	if Dead and Dead.Value == true then
		return true
	end
	local SDeath = Effects:FindFirstChild("SDeath")
	if SDeath and SDeath.Value == true then
		return true
	end
	return false
end

local function CanDamage(Character)
	if not Character then
		return false
	end
	local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not Humanoid or Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
		return false
	end
	if not Character:FindFirstChild("FULLY_LOADED_CHAR") then
		return false
	end
	if Character:FindFirstChild("FORCEFIELD") or Character:FindFirstChild("ForceField") then
		return false
	end
	if Character:FindFirstChild("GRABBING_CONSTRAINT") or Character:FindFirstChild("Christmas_Sock") then
		return false
	end
	if Character:GetAttribute("BasketballTeam") or Character:GetAttribute("BasketballZone") then
		return false
	end
	local Effects = Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
	if not Effects then
		return false
	end
	if GetFlag(Effects, "Cuff") == true then
		return false
	end
	if GetFlag(Effects, "K.O") == true then
		return false
	end
	return true
end

local function IsValid(Player)
	if Player == LocalPlayer then
		return false
	end
	local Character = Player.Character
	if not Character then
		return false
	end
	local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not Humanoid or Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
		return false
	end
	if not Character:FindFirstChild("FULLY_LOADED_CHAR") then
		return false
	end
	if Character:FindFirstChild("FORCEFIELD") or Character:FindFirstChild("ForceField") then
		return false
	end
	if Character:FindFirstChild("GRABBING_CONSTRAINT") then
		return false
	end
	if Config.FriendCheck and Friends[Player.UserId] then
		return false
	end
	return true
end

local IsKnocked

local function PickClosest(Root)
	local Best = nil
	local BestScore = nil
	for _, Player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if IsValid(Player) then
			if next(Config.Targets) and not Config.Targets[Player.Name] then
				continue
			end
			local TargetRoot = Player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if TargetRoot and not IsDead(Player.Character) then
				local Score
				if Config.TargetMode == "Health" then
					Score = Player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid").Health
				elseif Config.TargetMode == "Distance" then
					Score = (TargetRoot.Position - Root.Position).Magnitude
				else
					Score = (TargetRoot.Position - Camera.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
				end
				if Config.Targets[Player.Name] then
					Score = Score - 1e6
				end
				if IsKnocked(Player.Character) then
					Score = Score - 1e5
				end
				if not BestScore or Score < BestScore then
					Best = Player
					BestScore = Score
				end
			end
		end
	end
	return Best
end

local function TargetAlive(Player, AllowKnocked)
	if not Player or not Player.Parent then
		return false
	end
	local Character = Player.Character
	if not Character or not Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
		return false
	end
	if IsDead(Character) then
		return false
	end
	if not AllowKnocked and IsKnocked(Character) then
		return false
	end
	return true
end

local function FindTarget()
	local Root = GetRoot()
	if not Root then
		return nil
	end

	if StompVictim and TargetAlive(StompVictim, true) then
		return StompVictim
	end

	if Config.Revenge and RevengeTarget and TargetAlive(RevengeTarget, true) then
		return RevengeTarget
	end
	RevengeTarget = nil

	if LockedTarget and TargetAlive(LockedTarget, true) then
		local LockedCharacter = LockedTarget.Character
		if LockedCharacter and IsKnocked(LockedCharacter) then
			LockTime = os.clock()
			return LockedTarget
		end
		if os.clock() - LockTime < 8 then
			return LockedTarget
		end
	end
	LockedTarget = nil

	local Best = PickClosest(Root)

	if not Best and Config.TargetAim and next(Config.Targets) == nil then
		local AimPlayer = getgenv().FlameTargetAimPickCurrent and getgenv().FlameTargetAimPickCurrent()
		if AimPlayer and AimPlayer.Character and AimPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
			Best = AimPlayer
		end
	end

	LockedTarget = Best
	LockTime = os.clock()
	return Best
end

local function GetAmmo()
	local Data = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("DataFolder")
	local Inventory = Data and Data:FindFirstChild("Inventory")
	local Item = Inventory and Inventory:FindFirstChild("[Flamethrower]")
	if Item then
		return tonumber(Item.Value) or 0
	end
	return 0
end

local function GetCash()
	local Data = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("DataFolder")
	local Cash = Data and Data:FindFirstChild("Currency")
	if Cash then
		return tonumber(Cash.Value) or 0
	end
	return 0
end

local function GetHandle()
	local Tool = GetFlamethrower()
	if Tool then
		return Tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
	end
end

local function GetMag()
	local Tool = GetFlamethrower()
	if Tool then
		local Ammo = Tool:FindFirstChild("Ammo")
		if Ammo then
			return tonumber(Ammo.Value) or 0
		end
	end
	return 0
end

local function GetHealth()
	local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
	local Humanoid = Character and Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if Humanoid then
		return Humanoid.Health
	end
	return 0
end

local ArmorValue = nil
local ArmorCharacter = nil
local ArmorDropped = 0

local function WatchArmor(Character)
	ArmorCharacter = Character
	ArmorValue = nil
	if not Character then
		return
	end
	local Effects = Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
	local Armor = Effects and Effects:FindFirstChild("Armor")
	if Armor and Armor:IsA("ValueBase") then
		ArmorValue = Armor
		Armor:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Connect(function()
			ArmorDropped = os.clock()
		end)
	end
end

local function GetArmor()
	local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
	if ArmorCharacter ~= Character then
		WatchArmor(Character)
	end
	if ArmorValue and ArmorValue.Parent then
		return tonumber(ArmorValue.Value) or 0
	end
	local Effects = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
	local Armor = Effects and Effects:FindFirstChild("Armor")
	if Armor then
		return tonumber(Armor.Value) or 0
	end
end

local function GetFireArmor()
	local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
	local Effects = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
	local Fire = Effects and Effects:FindFirstChild("FireArmor")
	if Fire and Fire:IsA("ValueBase") then
		return tonumber(Fire.Value) or 0
	end
	return 0
end

local function IsReloading()
	local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
	local Effects = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
	local Reload = Effects and Effects:FindFirstChild("Reload")
	return Reload ~= nil and Reload.Value == true
end

IsKnocked = function(Character)
	if not Character then
		return false
	end
	local Effects = Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
	if Effects then
		local Knockout = Effects:FindFirstChild("K.O")
		if Knockout and Knockout.Value == true then
			return true
		end
	end
	return Character:GetAttribute("Knocked") == true
end

local function LocalReady(Character)
	if not Character then
		return false
	end
	local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not Humanoid or Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
		return false
	end
	if not Character:FindFirstChild("FULLY_LOADED_CHAR") then
		return false
	end
	if Character:FindFirstChild("FORCEFIELD") or Character:FindFirstChild("ForceField") then
		return false
	end
	if IsKnocked(Character) then
		return false
	end
	return true
end

local function HasKnife()
	local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
	if not Character then
		return nil
	end
	local Tool = Character:FindFirstChild("[Knife]")
	if Tool then
		return Tool
	end
	local Backpack = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
	if Backpack then
		return Backpack:FindFirstChild("[Knife]")
	end
	return nil
end

local function EquipKnife()
	local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
	if not Character then
		return
	end
	local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not Humanoid then
		return
	end
	local Backpack = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
	if Backpack then
		local Knife = Backpack:FindFirstChild("[Knife]")
		if Knife then
			Humanoid:EquipTool(Knife)
		end
	end
end

local function KnifeIsValid(Player)
	if Player == LocalPlayer then
		return false
	end
	local Character = Player.Character
	if not Character then
		return false
	end
	local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not Humanoid or Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
		return false
	end
	if not Character:FindFirstChild("FULLY_LOADED_CHAR") then
		return false
	end
	if Character:FindFirstChild("FORCEFIELD") or Character:FindFirstChild("ForceField") then
		return false
	end
	if Character:FindFirstChild("GRABBING_CONSTRAINT") then
		return false
	end
	if Config.KnifeFriendCheck and Friends[Player.UserId] then
		return false
	end
	return true
end

local function FindKnifeTarget(Root)
	if Config.Revenge and RevengeTarget and TargetAlive(RevengeTarget, true) then
		return RevengeTarget
	end
	if not Config.Revenge then
		RevengeTarget = nil
	end
	local Best = nil
	local BestScore = nil
	for _, Player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if KnifeIsValid(Player) then
			if next(Config.KnifeTargets) and not Config.KnifeTargets[Player.Name] then
				continue
			end
			local TargetRoot = Player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if TargetRoot and not IsDead(Player.Character) then
				local Score
				if Config.KnifeTargetMode == "Health" then
					Score = Player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid").Health
				elseif Config.KnifeTargetMode == "Distance" then
					Score = (TargetRoot.Position - Root.Position).Magnitude
				else
					Score = (TargetRoot.Position - Camera.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
				end
				if Config.KnifeTargets[Player.Name] then
					Score = Score - 1e6
				end
				if not BestScore or Score < BestScore then
					Best = Player
					BestScore = Score
				end
			end
		end
	end

	if not Best and Config.TargetAim and next(Config.KnifeTargets) == nil then
		local AimPlayer = getgenv().FlameTargetAimPickCurrent and getgenv().FlameTargetAimPickCurrent()
		if AimPlayer and AimPlayer.Character and AimPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
			Best = AimPlayer
		end
	end

	return Best
end

getgenv().FlameKnifeReachPosition = function(TargetRoot)
	return KnifeStrafePosition(TargetRoot.CFrame)
end

local KnifeKillOffsets = {
	Under = CFrame.new(0, -2, 0),
	Above = CFrame.new(0, 2.5, 0),
	Behind = CFrame.new(0, 0, 2),
	Front = CFrame.new(0, 0, -2),
}

local function KnifeStrafePosition(TargetCFrame)
	local Offset = KnifeKillOffsets[Config.KnifeFollowStyle] or KnifeKillOffsets.Behind
	return (TargetCFrame * Offset).Position
end

local function Reload(Tool)
	if not Tool or IsReloading() then
		return
	end
	if os.clock() - LastReload > ReloadDelay then
		LastReload = os.clock()
		pcall(function()
			MainEvent:FireServer("Reload", Tool)
		end)
	end
end

local function FireFlamethrower(Tool, TargetCharacter)
	local Handle = Tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
	if not Handle then
		return
	end
	local Hitbox = TargetCharacter:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or TargetCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or TargetCharacter:FindFirstChild("Torso")
	if not Hitbox then
		return
	end
	local Root = GetRoot()
	if not Root then
		return
	end
	local Origin = Root.Position
	local Pos = Hitbox.Position
	local Dir = (Pos - Origin)
	if Dir.Magnitude < 0.001 then
		Dir = (Handle.Position - Pos).Unit
	else
		Dir = Dir.Unit
	end
	pcall(function()
		MainEvent:FireServer("ShootGun", Handle, Origin, Pos, Hitbox, Dir)
	end)
end

local function ScanShop()
	ShopItems = {}
	local Ignored = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Ignored")
	local Shop = Ignored and Ignored:FindFirstChild("Shop")
	if not Shop then
		return
	end
	for _, Part in ipairs(Shop:GetChildren()) do
		local Amount, Name, Price = Part.Name:match("^(%d*)%s*%[(.-)%]%s*[%-%=]%s*%$(%d+)")
		if Name and Price then
			local Head = Part:FindFirstChild("Head")
			local Detector = Part:FindFirstChildOfClass("ClickDetector")
			if Head and Detector then
				local Key = string.lower(Name)
				local Cost = tonumber(Price)
				local Old = ShopItems[Key]
				if not Old or Cost < Old.Price then
					ShopItems[Key] = {
						Head = Head,
						Detector = Detector,
						Price = Cost,
						Amount = tonumber(Amount) or 1,
					}
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

local function PumpBuy(Name, Seconds, Stop, Read, Crouch, MaxClicks, StopOnChange, ClickDelay)
	local Info = ShopItems[Name]
	local Root = GetRoot()
	if not Info or not Root then
		return
	end
	if Crouch then
		SetCrouch(true)
	end
	local Pad = CFrame.new(Info.Head.Position - Vector3.new(0, 8.8, 0))
	local Stored = Root.CFrame
	local StoredVelocity = Root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
	pcall(function()
		sethiddenproperty(Root, "PhysicsRepRootRef", InstanceHandle.new(Info.Head))
	end)

	getgenv().FlameBuyHeartbeat = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if not Root.Parent then
			return
		end
		Root.CFrame = Pad
		Root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
	end)

	RunService:BindToRenderStep("FlameBuySync", 101, function()
		if Root.Parent then
			Root.CFrame = Stored
			Root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = StoredVelocity
		end
	end)

	local Settle = os.clock()
	while os.clock() - Settle < 0.3 do
		if not Root.Parent then
			break
		end
		RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
	end

	local Start = os.clock()
	local Limit = MaxClicks or 30
	local Base = Read and Read() or nil
	local Last = Base
	local Idle = 0
	local Clicks = 0
	while os.clock() - Start < Seconds and Clicks < Limit do
		if not Root.Parent then
			break
		end
		pcall(fireclickdetector, Info.Detector)
		Clicks = Clicks + 1
		if ClickDelay and ClickDelay > 0 then
			local NextClick = os.clock() + ClickDelay
			while os.clock() < NextClick and os.clock() - Start < Seconds do
				RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			end
		else
			RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
		end
		if Stop and Stop() then
			break
		end
		local Now = Read and Read() or nil
		if StopOnChange and Now ~= nil and Base ~= nil and Now ~= Base then
			break
		end
		if Now ~= nil and Last ~= nil and Now == Last then
			Idle = Idle + 1
			if Idle >= 3 and Clicks >= 6 then
				break
			end
		else
			Idle = 0
		end
		Last = Now
	end

	if getgenv().FlameBuyHeartbeat then
		getgenv().FlameBuyHeartbeat:Disconnect()
		getgenv().FlameBuyHeartbeat = nil
	end
	RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("FlameBuySync")
	pcall(function()
		sethiddenproperty(Root, "PhysicsRepRootRef", InstanceHandle.new(nil))
	end)
	pcall(function()
		if Root.Parent then
			Root.CFrame = Stored
			Root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = StoredVelocity
		end
	end)
	if Crouch then
		SetCrouch(false)
	end
end

local function BuyItem(Name, Seconds, Stop, Read, Crouch, MaxClicks, StopOnChange, ClickDelay)
	if BuyBusy then
		return
	end
	if not ShopItems[Name] then
		ScanShop()
	end
	if not ShopItems[Name] then
		return
	end
	BuyBusy = true
	LastBuy = os.clock()
	local Before = Read and Read() or nil
	task.spawn(function()
		pcall(PumpBuy, Name, Seconds or 1, Stop, Read, Crouch, MaxClicks, StopOnChange, ClickDelay)
		BuyBusy = false
		if Before ~= nil and Read and Read() == Before then
			BuyCooldown = os.clock() + 5
		end
	end)
end

local FoodIndex = 0

local function BuyFood()
	if BuyBusy then
		return
	end
	local Count = CountFood()
	if Count >= 2 then
		return
	end
	for _ = 1, #FoodNames do
		FoodIndex = FoodIndex % #FoodNames + 1
		local Name = FoodNames[FoodIndex]
		local Info = ShopItems[Name]
		if Info and GetCash() >= Info.Price then
			BuyItem(Name, 8, function()
				return CountFood() > Count
			end, CountFood, true, 30, true, Config.BuyClickDelay)
			return
		end
	end
end

local ArmorTiers = {
	["medium armor"] = 100,
	["high-medium armor"] = 130,
}

local ArmorPads = {
	["medium armor"] = true,
	["high-medium armor"] = true,
}

local function ArmorPadValue(Key)
	local Learned = Config.ArmorLearned
	local Known = Learned and Learned[Key]
	if Known and Known > 0 then
		return Known
	end
	return ArmorTiers[Key]
end

local function PickArmorPad()
	local Have = GetArmor() or 0
	if not Config.BuyBestPad then
		if Have < 100 then
			return "medium armor"
		end
		return nil
	end
	local Target = Config.ArmorAmount or 0
	if Have >= Target then
		return nil
	end
	local Cash = GetCash()
	local Reserve = Config.CashReserve or 15000
	local Reach, ReachPrice = nil, math.huge
	local Best, BestValue, BestPrice = nil, Have, math.huge
	for Key, Info in pairs(ShopItems) do
		if ArmorPads[Key] and Cash >= Info.Price then
			local Value = ArmorPadValue(Key)
			if Value and Value > Have then
				if Value >= Target and Info.Price < ReachPrice then
					Reach, ReachPrice = Key, Info.Price
				end
				if Value > BestValue or (Value == BestValue and Info.Price < BestPrice) then
					Best, BestValue, BestPrice = Key, Value, Info.Price
				end
			end
		end
	end
	return Reach or Best
end

getgenv().FlameOwnsShopItem = function(Name)
	if not Name or Name == "" then
		return false
	end

	local Want = string.lower(Name)

	local function Scan(Container)
		if not Container then
			return false
		end
		for _, Child in ipairs(Container:GetChildren()) do
			local Lower = string.lower(Child.Name)
			if Lower == Want or Lower == "[" .. Want .. "]" then
				return true
			end
		end
		return false
	end

	if Scan(LocalPlayer.Character) then
		return true
	end

	if Scan(LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")) then
		return true
	end

	local DataFolder = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("DataFolder")
	local Inventory = DataFolder and DataFolder:FindFirstChild("Inventory")

	return Scan(Inventory)
end

local function EnsureLoadout()
	if BuyBusy then
		return
	end
	local Now = os.clock()
	if Now - LastBuy < 1.5 or Now < BuyCooldown then
		return
	end
	if not LocalPlayer.Character then
		return
	end
	local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
	if Character:FindFirstChild("FORCEFIELD") or Character:FindFirstChild("ForceField") then
		SpawnBuyUntil = math.max(SpawnBuyUntil, os.clock() + 1)
	end
	if SpawnBuyUntil > 0 and os.clock() > SpawnBuyUntil then
		SpawnBuyUntil = 0
		SpawnBuyingDone = true
	end
	if SpawnBuyingDone and SpawnBuyUntil == 0 and not (Character:FindFirstChild("FORCEFIELD") or Character:FindFirstChild("ForceField")) then
		local needBuy = false
		if Config.AutoArmor and (GetArmor() or 0) < Config.ArmorAmount then needBuy = true end
		if Config.AutoFireArmor and GetFireArmor() < Config.FireArmorAmount then needBuy = true end
		if (Config.AutoAmmo or Config.Enabled) and OwnsFlamethrower() and GetAmmo() <= 0 then needBuy = true end
		if Config.Enabled and not Config.KnifeEnabled and not OwnsFlamethrower() then needBuy = true end
		if Config.AutoHeal and Config.AutoFood and GetHealth() <= Config.HealAmount and not GetFood() then needBuy = true end
		if Config.AutoBuy and Config.AutoBuyItem ~= "" and not getgenv().FlameOwnsShopItem(Config.AutoBuyItem) then needBuy = true end
		if not needBuy then return end
	end
	local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not Humanoid or Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
		return
	end
	if Config.AutoBuy and Config.AutoBuyItem ~= "" then
		if not next(ShopItems) then
			ScanShop()
		end
		local Info = ShopItems[Config.AutoBuyItem]
		if Info then
			if getgenv().FlameOwnsShopItem(Config.AutoBuyItem) then
				Config.AutoBuy = false
				Library:Notification({ Name = "auto buy: already own " .. Config.AutoBuyItem, Time = 4 })
				return
			end
			if GetCash() >= Info.Price then
				BuyItem(Config.AutoBuyItem, 12, function()
					return getgenv().FlameOwnsShopItem(Config.AutoBuyItem)
				end, GetCash, false, 12, true, Config.BuyClickDelay)
				return
			end
		end
	end
	if Config.AutoArmor then
		local Have = GetArmor() or 0
		if Have < Config.ArmorAmount then
			local Pad = PickArmorPad()
			local Info = Pad and ShopItems[Pad]
			if Pad and Info and GetCash() >= Info.Price then
				BuyItem(Pad, 10, function()
					return (GetArmor() or 0) >= Config.ArmorAmount
				end, GetArmor, false, 30, true, Config.BuyClickDelay)
				return
			end
		end
	end
	if Config.AutoMask then
		local Backpack = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
		local Mask = (Character and Character:FindFirstChild("[Mask]")) or (Backpack and Backpack:FindFirstChild("[Mask]"))
		if not Mask then
			BuyItem("ninja mask", 8, function()
				local Bp = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
				return (Bp and Bp:FindFirstChild("[Mask]")) ~= nil
			end, nil, false, 30, true, Config.BuyClickDelay)
			return
		elseif Mask.Parent ~= Character and Humanoid then
			pcall(function()
				Humanoid:EquipTool(Mask)
				Mask:Activate()
			end)
		end
	end
	if Config.Enabled and not Config.KnifeEnabled and not OwnsFlamethrower() then
		if GetCash() >= 11000 then
			BuyItem("flamethrower", 12, function()
				return GetFlamethrower() ~= nil
			end, GetFlamethrower, false, 30, true, Config.BuyClickDelay)
			return
		end
	end
	if IsKnocked(Character) then
		return
	end
	if Config.AutoHeal and Config.AutoFood then
		local Health = GetHealth()
		if Health > 0 and Health < Humanoid.MaxHealth and Health <= Config.HealAmount then
			if GetFood() then
				if Now - LastEat > 1 then
					LastEat = Now
					EatFood()
				end
				return
			end
			if Now - LastEat > 1 then
				LastEat = Now
				BuyFood()
			end
			return
		end
	end
	if Config.AutoArmor then
		local Armor = GetArmor() or 0
		if Armor < Config.ArmorAmount then
			if Now < BuyCooldown and Now - ArmorDropped > 1.5 then
				return
			end
			local Pad = PickArmorPad()
			if Pad then
				BuyItem(Pad, 3, function()
					local Have = GetArmor() or 0
					if Have > Armor then
						Config.ArmorLearned[Pad] = Have
						Armor = Have
					end
					return Have >= Config.ArmorAmount
				end, GetArmor, false, 30, true, Config.BuyClickDelay)
				return
			end
		end
	end
	if Config.AutoFireArmor then
		local Fire = GetFireArmor()
		if Fire < Config.FireArmorAmount then
			local Info = ShopItems["fire armor"]
			if Info and GetCash() >= Info.Price then
				BuyItem("fire armor", 10, function()
					return GetFireArmor() >= Config.FireArmorAmount
				end, GetFireArmor, false, 30, true, Config.BuyClickDelay)
				return
			end
		end
	end
	if GetCash() < 20000 then
		return
	end
	if (Config.AutoAmmo or Config.Enabled) and OwnsFlamethrower() and GetAmmo() <= 0 then
		BuyItem("flamethrower ammo", 12, nil, GetAmmo, false, 10, false, Config.BuyClickDelay)
	end
end

local function LoadoutReady()
	local Tool = GetFlamethrower()
	if not Tool then
		return false
	end
	local Mag = Tool:FindFirstChild("Ammo")
	if GetAmmo() <= 0 and (not Mag or Mag.Value <= 0) then
		return false
	end
	local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
	local Humanoid = Character and Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not Humanoid or Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
		return false
	end
	if Config.AutoHeal and Humanoid.Health <= Config.HealAmount then
		return false
	end
	if Config.AutoArmor and (GetArmor() or 0) < Config.ArmorAmount then
		return false
	end
	return true
end

local function UpdateFriends()
	for _, Player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if Player ~= LocalPlayer then
			task.spawn(function()
				local Ok, Result = pcall(function()
					return LocalPlayer:IsFriendsWith(Player.UserId)
				end)
				Friends[Player.UserId] = Ok and Result == true
			end)
		end
	end
end

Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(Player)
	task.spawn(function()
		local Ok, Result = pcall(function()
			return LocalPlayer:IsFriendsWith(Player.UserId)
		end)
		Friends[Player.UserId] = Ok and Result == true
	end)
end)

Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(Player)
	Friends[Player.UserId] = nil
end)


if getgenv().FlameHitDraws then
	for _, Object in ipairs(getgenv().FlameHitDraws) do
		pcall(function()
			Object:Remove()
		end)
	end
	getgenv().FlameHitDraws = nil
end

local SOUNDS = {}
local SoundNames = {}
for Index, Entry in ipairs(SoundList) do
	SOUNDS[Entry[1]] = "rbxassetid://" .. Entry[2]
	SoundNames[Index] = Entry[1]
end

for _, Name in ipairs({ "FlameHitSound", "FlameAmbience" }) do
	local Previous = SoundService:FindFirstChild(Name)
	if Previous then
		Previous:Destroy()
	end
end

local HOLDER = Instance.new("Folder")

local HitEffectModule = { Locals = { Type = {} }, Functions = {} }

HitEffectModule.Settings = { HitEffect = { Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) } }

local utility = {}

function utility.instance_new(cls, props)
	local inst = Instance.new(cls)
	local parent = nil
	for k, v in next, props do
		if k == "Parent" then
			parent = v
		else
			inst[k] = v
		end
	end
	if parent then inst.Parent = parent end
	return inst
end

do
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	part.Parent = HOLDER
	local attachment = Instance.new("Attachment")
	attachment.Name = "Attachment"
	attachment.Parent = part
	HitEffectModule.Locals.Type["Nova"] = attachment

	local function createParticleEmitter(acceleration)
		local emitter = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
		emitter.Name = "ParticleEmitter"
		emitter.Acceleration = acceleration
		emitter.Color = ColorSequence.new({
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.495, HitEffectModule.Settings.HitEffect.Color),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0))
		})
		emitter.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.5, 0.5)
		emitter.LightEmission = 1
		emitter.LockedToPart = true
		emitter.Rate = 1
		emitter.Rotation = NumberRange.new(0, 360)
		emitter.Size = NumberSequence.new({
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 10),
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
		})
		emitter.Speed = NumberRange.new(0, 0)
		emitter.Texture = "rbxassetid://1084991215"
		emitter.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0),
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.1),
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.534, 0.25),
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.5),
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0)
		})
		emitter.ZOffset = 1
		emitter.Parent = attachment
		return emitter
	end

	createParticleEmitter(Vector3.new(0, 0, 1))
	local perpendicularEmitter = createParticleEmitter(Vector3.new(0, 1, -0.001))
	perpendicularEmitter.Orientation = Enum.ParticleOrientation.VelocityPerpendicular
end

local hit_effects = {}

			Color = ColorSequence.new{ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,Color3.new(0,0,0)),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,Color3.new(0,0,0))};

			LockedToPart = true;
			Orientation = Enum.ParticleOrientation.VelocityParallel;
			Rate = 0;
			Rotation = NumberRange.new(-90,90);
			Size = NumberSequence.new{NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,1,0),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,8.5,1.5)};
			Speed = NumberRange.new(0.1,0.1);
			SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(180,180);
			Texture = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=6820680001";
			Transparency = NumberSequence.new{NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,1,0),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.200791,0,0),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.699605,0,0),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,1,0)};
			ZOffset = 1.5;
			Parent = part
		});

		particle1:Emit(1);
		particle2:Emit(1);
		particle3:Emit(1);

		task.delay(1, function()
			part:Destroy();
		end);
	end;
end;

hit_effects.Type = HitEffectModule.Locals.Type
hit_effects.Names = {
	"Crescent Slash",
	"Cosmic Explosion",
	"Coom",
	"Slash",
	"Atomic Slash",
	"AuraBurst",
	"Thunder",
	"Nova",

	Source = nil
	if Chunk then
		local Ok, Module = pcall(Chunk)
		if Ok and type(Module) == "table" then
			HitFxModule = Module
		end
	end
	if HitFxModule and HitFxModule.Holder then
		HitFxModule.Holder.Name = "FlameHitFxAssets"
	end
