local HitFxNames = (HitFxModule and HitFxModule.Names) or {}

local HitDraws = {}

local HitCirclePool = {}
local HitCrossPool = {}
for Index = 1, 12 do
	local Circle = Drawing.new("Circle")
	Circle.Visible = false
	Circle.Thickness = 1.5
	Circle.NumSides = 24
	Circle.Filled = false
	HitCirclePool[Index] = Circle
	HitDraws[#HitDraws + 1] = Circle
	local LineA = Drawing.new("Line")
	local LineB = Drawing.new("Line")
	LineA.Visible = false
	LineA.Thickness = 1.5
	LineB.Visible = false
	LineB.Thickness = 1.5
	HitCrossPool[Index] = { LineA, LineB }
	HitDraws[#HitDraws + 1] = LineA
	HitDraws[#HitDraws + 1] = LineB
end

local HitChamsBones = {
	"Head", "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso",
	"LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand",
	"RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm", "RightHand",
	"LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot",
	"RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot",
}

local HitChamsMaterials = {
	Neon = Enum.Material.Neon,
	ForceField = Enum.Material.ForceField,
	SmoothPlastic = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic,
	Glass = Enum.Material.Glass,
}

local HitDamagePool = {}
for Index = 1, 12 do
	local Text = Drawing.new("Text")
	Text.Visible = false
	Text.Center = true
	Text.Outline = true
	Text.Size = 16
	Text.Font = 2
	Text.ZIndex = 3
	HitDamagePool[Index] = Text
	HitDraws[#HitDraws + 1] = Text
end

getgenv().FlameHitDraws = HitDraws

local HitMarks = {}
local HitHidden = false

local function WorldToScreen(Position)
	local Point, OnScreen = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(Position)
	if not OnScreen or Point.Z <= 0 then
		return nil
	end
	return Vector2.new(Point.X, Point.Y)
end

local function PlayHitSound()
	local Id = SOUNDS[Config.HitSoundName]
	if not Id then
		return
	end
	HitSoundObject.SoundId = Id
	HitSoundObject.Volume = math.clamp(Config.HitVolume, 0, 10)
	pcall(function()
		HitSoundObject:Play()
	end)
end

local function PlayAmbience()
	if AmbientSound then
		AmbientSound:Stop()
		AmbientSound:Destroy()
		AmbientSound = nil
	end
	if not Config.Ambience then
		return
	end
	local Id = AmbienceIds[Config.AmbienceSound]
	if not Id then
		return
	end
	AmbientSound = Instance.new("Sound")
	AmbientSound.Name = "FlameAmbience"
	AmbientSound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://" .. Id
	AmbientSound.Looped = true
	AmbientSound.Volume = Config.AmbienceVolume
	AmbientSound.Parent = SoundService
	pcall(function()
		AmbientSound:Play()
	end)
end

local function DoHitChams(Character)
	if not Config.HitChamsEnabled or not Character then
		return
	end
	local Colour = Config.HitChamsColor
	local Material = HitChamsMaterials[Config.HitChamsMat] or Enum.Material.Neon
	local Start = Config.HitChamsTr
	local Duration = Config.HitChamsDur
	local Fade = Config.HitChamsFade
	local Ghosts = {}
	for _, Bone in ipairs(HitChamsBones) do
		local Part = Character:FindFirstChild(Bone, true)
		if Part and Part:IsA("BasePart") then
			local Ghost = Instance.new("Part")
			Ghost.Anchored = true
			Ghost.CanCollide = false
			Ghost.CanQuery = false
			Ghost.CanTouch = false
			Ghost.Size = Part.Size
			Ghost.CFrame = Part.CFrame
			Ghost.Transparency = Start
			Ghost.Color = Colour
			Ghost.Material = Material
			Ghost.Parent = Workspace.Terrain
			Ghosts[#Ghosts + 1] = Ghost
		end
	end
	task.spawn(function()
		if Fade then
			local Info = TweenInfo.new(Duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear)
			for _, Ghost in ipairs(Ghosts) do
				TweenService:Create(Ghost, Info, { Transparency = 1 }):Play()
			end
		end
		task.wait(Duration)
		for _, Ghost in ipairs(Ghosts) do
			pcall(function()
				Ghost:Destroy()
			end)
		end
	end)
end

local function PlayParticleEffect(Name, Character, Position)
	if not HitFxModule then
		return
	end
	local Proto = HitFxModule.Type and HitFxModule.Type[Name]
	if not Proto then
		return
	end
	local Host = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local Temp = nil
	if not Host and Position then
		Temp = Instance.new("Part")
		Temp.Anchored = true
		Temp.CanCollide = false
		Temp.Transparency = 1
		Temp.Size = Vector3.new(0.1, 0.1, 0.1)
		Temp.Position = Position
		Temp.Parent = Workspace
		Host = Temp
	end
	if not Host then
		return
	end
	local Clone = Proto:Clone()
	Clone.Parent = Host
	local Black = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
	for _, Emitter in ipairs(Clone:GetChildren()) do
		if Emitter:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
			if Config.HitFxRecolour then
				Emitter.Color = ColorSequence.new({
					ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Black),
					ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.495, Config.HitFxColor),
					ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Black),
				})
			end
			pcall(function()
				Emitter:Emit()
			end)
		end
	end
	task.delay(math.clamp(Config.HitFxDur, 0.2, 10), function()
		pcall(function()
			Clone:Destroy()
		end)
		if Temp then
			pcall(function()
				Temp:Destroy()
			end)
		end
	end)
end

local ShotFired = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
local ShotHit = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
local LastMiss = 0

local function FormatHit(Text, Fallback, Name, Part, Damage, Health)
	local Out = Text
	if Out == nil or Out == "" then
		Out = Fallback
	end
	Out = string.gsub(Out, "{NAME}", tostring(Name or ""))
	Out = string.gsub(Out, "{PART}", tostring(Part or ""))
	Out = string.gsub(Out, "{DMG}", tostring(Damage or 0))
	Out = string.gsub(Out, "{HP}", tostring(Health or 0))
	return Out
end

local function ShowHit(Character, Player, Damage, Position, Bone, Health)
	if Config.HitEnabled then
		local Pick = Config.HitEffects
	for Name in pairs(Pick) do
		if Name == "Circle" or Name == "Cross" then
			HitMarks[#HitMarks + 1] = { Position = Position, Time = os.clock(), Mode = Name }
			if #HitMarks > 12 then
				table.remove(HitMarks, 1)
			end
		else
			PlayParticleEffect(Name, Character, Position)
		end
	end
		if Config.HitChamsEnabled then
			DoHitChams(Character)
		end
	end
	if Config.HitSound then
		PlayHitSound()
	end
	if Config.HitNotify then
		local Body
		if Damage < 1 then
			Body = FormatHit(Config.HitZeroText, "Hit {NAME} in the {PART} for 0 damage", PlayerTag(Player), Bone, Damage, Health)
		else
			Body = FormatHit(Config.HitText, "Hit {NAME} in the {PART} for {DMG} damage", PlayerTag(Player), Bone, Damage, Health)
		end
		Library:Notification({ Name = Body, Time = Config.HitNotifDuration })
	end
end

local function WatchHits()
	local Now = os.clock()
	for _, Player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if Player ~= LocalPlayer then
			local Character = Player.Character
			local Humanoid = Character and Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if Humanoid then
				local Health = Humanoid.Health
				local Previous = HitHealth[Character]
				HitHealth[Character] = Health
				if Previous and Health < Previous - 0.5 then
					local Mine = false
					local Effects = Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
					local Damager = Effects and Effects:FindFirstChild("LastDamager")
					if Damager and PlayerFromValue(Damager.Value) == LocalPlayer then
						Mine = true
					elseif HitClaim[Player] and Now - HitClaim[Player] <= 0.6 then
						Mine = true
					end
					if Mine then
						local Bone = Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						if Bone then
							local Lost = Previous - Health
							if Now - (HitLastFx[Player] or 0) >= 0.1 then
								HitLastFx[Player] = Now
								ShotHit[Player] = true
								ShowHit(Character, Player, math.floor(Lost + 0.5), Bone.Position, Bone.Name, Health)
							end
							if Config.HitDamage then
								local Entry = DamageMarks[Player]
								if not Entry then
									Entry = { Total = 0, Position = Bone.Position, Time = Now }
									DamageMarks[Player] = Entry
								end
								Entry.Total = Entry.Total + Lost
								Entry.Position = Bone.Position
								Entry.Time = Now
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	if Config.HitNotify and Config.HitMissText ~= "" then
		for Player, Fired in pairs(ShotFired) do
			if Now - Fired > 0.4 then
				ShotFired[Player] = nil
				if not ShotHit[Player] and Now - LastMiss > 1.5 then
					LastMiss = Now
					Library:Notification({ Name = FormatHit(Config.HitMissText, "Missed shot due to config issue & being a nn", PlayerTag(Player), "air", 0, 0), Time = Config.HitNotifDuration })
				end
				ShotHit[Player] = nil
			end
		end
	end
end

local function DrawHits()
	local Now = os.clock()
	local Life = math.max(0.05, Config.HitLife)
	local Colour = Config.HitColor

	local UsedCircles = 0
	local UsedCrosses = 0
	if #HitMarks == 0 then
		if not HitHidden then
			HitHidden = true
			for Index = 1, #HitCirclePool do
				HitCirclePool[Index].Visible = false
				HitCrossPool[Index][1].Visible = false
				HitCrossPool[Index][2].Visible = false
			end
		end
	else
		HitHidden = false
		for Index = #HitMarks, 1, -1 do
			local Mark = HitMarks[Index]
			local Age = Now - Mark.Time
			if Age > Life then
				table.remove(HitMarks, Index)
			else
				local Point = WorldToScreen(Mark.Position)
				if Point then
					local Alpha = 1 - (Age / Life)
					if Mark.Mode == "Cross" then
						UsedCrosses = UsedCrosses + 1
						if UsedCrosses <= #HitCrossPool then
							local Pair = HitCrossPool[UsedCrosses]
							local Half = Config.HitSize
							Pair[1].From = Vector2.new(Point.X - Half, Point.Y - Half)
							Pair[1].To = Vector2.new(Point.X + Half, Point.Y + Half)
							Pair[2].From = Vector2.new(Point.X - Half, Point.Y + Half)
							Pair[2].To = Vector2.new(Point.X + Half, Point.Y - Half)
							for _, Line in ipairs(Pair) do
								Line.Color = Colour
								Line.Transparency = Alpha
								Line.Visible = true
							end
						end
					else
						UsedCircles = UsedCircles + 1
						if UsedCircles <= #HitCirclePool then
							local Circle = HitCirclePool[UsedCircles]
							Circle.Position = Point
							Circle.Color = Colour
							Circle.Radius = Config.HitSize * (1 + (1 - Alpha) * 0.6)
							Circle.Transparency = Alpha
							Circle.Visible = true
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		for Index = UsedCircles + 1, #HitCirclePool do
			HitCirclePool[Index].Visible = false
		end
		for Index = UsedCrosses + 1, #HitCrossPool do
			HitCrossPool[Index][1].Visible = false
			HitCrossPool[Index][2].Visible = false
		end
	end

	local Used = 0
	if Config.HitDamage then
		for Player, Entry in pairs(DamageMarks) do
			local Idle = Now - Entry.Time
			if Idle > 1.2 then
				DamageMarks[Player] = nil
			elseif Used < #HitDamagePool then
				local Point = WorldToScreen(Entry.Position)
				if Point then
					Used = Used + 1
					local Text = HitDamagePool[Used]
					Text.Text = "-" .. math.floor(Entry.Total + 0.5)
					Text.Position = Point - Vector2.new(0, Idle * 30)
					Text.Color = Config.HitDamageColor
					Text.Size = Config.HitDamageSize
					Text.Transparency = Idle > 0.6 and (1 - ((Idle - 0.6) / 0.6)) or 1
					Text.Visible = true
				end
			end
		end
	end
	for Index = Used + 1, #HitDamagePool do
		HitDamagePool[Index].Visible = false
	end
end

pcall(function()
	RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("FlameHitFx")
end)
RunService:BindToRenderStep("FlameHitFx", 101, DrawHits)

if getgenv().FlameHitLoop then
	getgenv().FlameHitLoop:Disconnect()
end
getgenv().FlameHitLoop = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(WatchHits)

local SavedSky = nil
local SavedSkyName = nil
local OwnSky = nil
local SavedLight = nil
local OwnCC = nil
local TextureConn = nil
local Touched = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

local function CaptureSky()
	if SavedSky then
		return
	end
	local Sky = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Sky")
	if not Sky then
		return
	end
	SavedSkyName = Sky.Name
	SavedSky = {}
	for _, Prop in ipairs(SKY_PROPS) do
		local Ok, Value = pcall(function()
			return Sky[Prop]
		end)
		if Ok then
			SavedSky[Prop] = Value
		end
	end
end

local function ApplySkybox()
	pcall(function()
		CaptureSky()
		local Sky = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Sky")
		if not (Config.WorldVisuals and Config.Skybox) then
			if OwnSky then
				OwnSky:Destroy()
				OwnSky = nil
			end
			if Sky and SavedSky then
				for Prop, Value in pairs(SavedSky) do
					pcall(function()
						Sky[Prop] = Value
					end)
				end
				if SavedSkyName then
					pcall(function()
						Sky.Name = SavedSkyName
					end)
				end
			end
			return
		end
		local Def = OH_SKYBOXES[Config.SkyboxName]
		if not Def then
			return
		end
		if Def._custom then
			local Raw = string.gsub(tostring(Config.SkyboxCustom or ""), "%D", "")
			if Raw == "" then
				return
			end
			local Url = "rbxassetid://" .. Raw
			Def = {
				SkyboxBk = Url,
				SkyboxDn = Url,
				SkyboxFt = Url,
				SkyboxLf = Url,
				SkyboxRt = Url,
				SkyboxUp = Url,
				StarCount = 0,
				SunAngularSize = 0,
				MoonAngularSize = 0,
				CelestialBodiesShown = false,
			}
		end
		if not Sky then
			if not OwnSky or not OwnSky.Parent then
				OwnSky = Instance.new("Sky")
				OwnSky.Name = "FlameSky"
				OwnSky.Parent = Lighting
			end
			Sky = OwnSky
		end
		for _, Prop in ipairs(SKY_PROPS) do
			if Def[Prop] ~= nil then
				pcall(function()
					Sky[Prop] = Def[Prop]
				end)
			end
		end
	end)
end

local function CaptureLight()
	if SavedLight then
		return
	end
	SavedLight = {}
	for _, Prop in ipairs(LIGHT_PROPS) do
		local Ok, Value = pcall(function()
			return Lighting[Prop]
		end)
		if Ok then
			SavedLight[Prop] = Value
		end
	end
end

local function ApplyLighting()
	pcall(function()
		CaptureLight()
		if not (Config.WorldVisuals and Config.Lighting) then
			if SavedLight then
				for Prop, Value in pairs(SavedLight) do
					pcall(function()
						Lighting[Prop] = Value
					end)
				end
			end
			return
		end
		Lighting.Brightness = Config.Brightness
		Lighting.FogStart = Config.FogStart
		Lighting.FogEnd = Config.FogEnd
		Lighting.FogColor = Config.FogColor
		Lighting.Ambient = Config.Ambient
		Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Config.OutdoorAmbient
	end)
end

local function ApplyColour()
	pcall(function()
		if not (Config.WorldVisuals and Config.ColourCorrection) then
			if OwnCC then
				OwnCC:Destroy()
				OwnCC = nil
			end
			return
		end
		if not OwnCC or not OwnCC.Parent then
			OwnCC = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
			OwnCC.Name = "FlameColour"
			OwnCC.Parent = Lighting
		end
		OwnCC.Brightness = Config.CCBrightness
		OwnCC.Contrast = Config.CCContrast
		OwnCC.Saturation = Config.CCSaturation
		OwnCC.TintColor = Config.CCTint
	end)
end

local function IsCharacterPart(Part)
	local Parent = Part.Parent
	if not Parent then
		return true
	end
	if Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
		return true
	end
	if Part:IsDescendantOf(Lighting) then
		return true
	end
	local Viewmodels = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Viewmodels")
	if Viewmodels and Part:IsDescendantOf(Viewmodels) then
		return true
	end
	return false
end

local function StripTextures(Part)
	for _, Child in ipairs(Part:GetChildren()) do
		if Child:IsA("Texture") and Child.Name == "FlameTexture" then
			Child:Destroy()
		end
	end
end

local function StylePart(Part)
	if not Part:IsA("BasePart") or IsCharacterPart(Part) then
		return
	end
	local Pack = OH_TEXTURE_PACKS[Config.TexturePack] or OH_TEXTURE_PACKS.Minecraft
	if not Pack then
		return
	end
	local Saved = Touched[Part]
	local Name = Saved and Saved.Name or Part.Material.Name
	local Id = Pack[Name]
	if not Id then
		return
	end
	if not Saved then
		Touched[Part] = { Material = Part.Material, Name = Part.Material.Name }
	end
	StripTextures(Part)
	for _, Face in ipairs(FACES) do
		local Tex = Instance.new("Texture")
		Tex.Name = "FlameTexture"
		Tex.ZIndex = 2147483647
		Tex.Texture = "rbxassetid://" .. Id
		Tex.Face = Enum.NormalId[Face]
		Tex.Color3 = Part.Color
		Tex.Transparency = Part.Transparency
		Tex.Parent = Part
	end
	Part.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
end

local function ApplyTextures(Repaint)
	pcall(function()
		if not Config.Textures then
			if TextureConn then
				TextureConn:Disconnect()
				TextureConn = nil
			end
			for Part, Saved in pairs(Touched) do
				if Part.Parent then
					pcall(function()
						StripTextures(Part)
						Part.Material = Saved.Material
					end)
				end
			end
			table.clear(Touched)
			return
		end
		if Repaint then
			for Part in pairs(Touched) do
				if Part.Parent then
					pcall(StripTextures, Part)
				end
			end
		end
		for _, Part in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
			StylePart(Part)
		end
		if not TextureConn then
			TextureConn = Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(Part)
				if Config.Textures and Part:IsA("BasePart") then
					task.defer(StylePart, Part)
				end
			end)
		end
	end)
end

local CHAM_MATERIAL = Enum.Material.ForceField

local ChamsSaved = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
local ChamsChar = nil

local function ClearChams()
	for Part, Saved in pairs(ChamsSaved) do
		if Part.Parent then
			pcall(function()
				Part.Material = Saved.Material
				Part.Color = Saved.Color
			end)
		end
	end
	table.clear(ChamsSaved)
	ChamsChar = nil
end

local function CollectChams()
	local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
	if not Character then
		return nil
	end
	local Parts = {}

	if Config.SelfChams then
		for _, Part in ipairs(Character:GetChildren()) do
			if Part:IsA("BasePart") then
				Parts[#Parts + 1] = { Part = Part, Colour = Config.SelfChamsColour and Config.SelfChamsColor or nil }
			end
		end
	end

	if Config.ToolChams then
		local Tool = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
		if Tool then
			for _, Part in ipairs(Tool:GetDescendants()) do
				if Part:IsA("BasePart") then
					Parts[#Parts + 1] = { Part = Part, Colour = Config.ToolChamsColour and Config.ToolChamsColor or nil }
				end
			end
		end
	end

	if Config.AccChams then
		for _, Item in ipairs(Character:GetChildren()) do
			if Item:IsA("Accoutrement") then
				for _, Part in ipairs(Item:GetDescendants()) do
					if Part:IsA("BasePart") then
						Parts[#Parts + 1] = { Part = Part, Colour = Config.AccChamsColour and Config.AccChamsColor or nil }
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return Parts
end

local function ApplyChams()
	if not (Config.SelfChams or Config.ToolChams or Config.AccChams) then
		if ChamsChar then
			ClearChams()
		end
		return
	end

	local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
	if not Character then
		return
	end

	if Character ~= ChamsChar then
		ClearChams()
		ChamsChar = Character
	end

	local Entries = CollectChams()
	if not Entries then
		return
	end

	local Wanted = {}
	for _, Entry in ipairs(Entries) do
		Wanted[Entry.Part] = true
	end

	for Part, Saved in pairs(ChamsSaved) do
		if not Wanted[Part] then
			if Part.Parent then
				pcall(function()
					Part.Material = Saved.Material
					Part.Color = Saved.Color
				end)
			end
			ChamsSaved[Part] = nil
		end
	end

	for _, Entry in ipairs(Entries) do
		local Part = Entry.Part
		local Saved = ChamsSaved[Part]

		if not Saved then
			Saved = { Material = Part.Material, Color = Part.Color }
			ChamsSaved[Part] = Saved
		end

		if Part.Material ~= CHAM_MATERIAL then
			Part.Material = CHAM_MATERIAL
		end

		local Target = Entry.Colour or Saved.Color

		if Part.Color ~= Target then
			Part.Color = Target
		end
	end
end

local ChinaHatDraws = {}

local function ClearHat()
	for _, Pair in ipairs(ChinaHatDraws) do
		pcall(function()
			Pair[1]:Remove()
		end)
		pcall(function()
			Pair[2]:Remove()
		end)
	end
	ChinaHatDraws = {}
	getgenv().FlameChinaHatDraws = ChinaHatDraws

	local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
	local Head = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
	if Head then
		for _, Child in ipairs(Head:GetChildren()) do
			if Child.Name == "ConeHat" then
				pcall(function()
					Child:Destroy()
				end)
			end
		end
	end
end

local function BuildHat()
	local Sides = math.max(3, math.floor(Config.ChinaHatSides))
	if #ChinaHatDraws == Sides then
		return
	end
	ClearHat()
	for _ = 1, Sides do
		local Line = Drawing.new("Line")
		Line.ZIndex = 2
		Line.Thickness = 1
		local Triangle = Drawing.new("Triangle")
		Triangle.ZIndex = 1
		Triangle.Filled = true
		ChinaHatDraws[#ChinaHatDraws + 1] = { Line, Triangle }
	end
	getgenv().FlameChinaHatDraws = ChinaHatDraws
end

local function HatColour(Progress)
	local Shift = (Progress + os.clock() * Config.ChinaHatSpeed) % 1
	local C1 = Config.ChinaHatColour1
	local C2 = Config.ChinaHatColour2
	local C3 = Config.ChinaHatColour3
	local C4 = Config.ChinaHatColour4
	if Shift < 0.25 then
		return C1:Lerp(C2, Shift / 0.25)
	elseif Shift < 0.5 then
		return C2:Lerp(C3, (Shift - 0.25) / 0.25)
	elseif Shift < 0.75 then
		return C3:Lerp(C4, (Shift - 0.5) / 0.25)
	end
	return C4:Lerp(C1, (Shift - 0.75) / 0.25)
end

local function RenderHat()
	if not Config.ChinaHat or #ChinaHatDraws == 0 then
		return
	end

	local Sides = #ChinaHatDraws
	local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
	local Head = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
	local Camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera

	if not Head or not Camera then
		for Index = 1, Sides do
			ChinaHatDraws[Index][1].Visible = false
			ChinaHatDraws[Index][2].Visible = false
		end
		return
	end

	local Top = Head.Position + Vector3.new(0, Config.ChinaHatOffsetY + Config.ChinaHatHeight, 0)
	local Base = Head.Position + Vector3.new(0, Config.ChinaHatOffsetY, 0)
	local TopScreen, TopOn = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(Top)
	local HeadScreen, HeadOn = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(Head.Position)

	if not TopOn or not HeadOn or HeadScreen.Z <= 0 then
		for Index = 1, Sides do
			ChinaHatDraws[Index][1].Visible = false
			ChinaHatDraws[Index][2].Visible = false
		end
		return
	end

	local TopPoint = Vector2.new(TopScreen.X, TopScreen.Y)
	local Full = math.pi * 2

	for Index = 1, Sides do
		local Line = ChinaHatDraws[Index][1]
		local Triangle = ChinaHatDraws[Index][2]
		local P1 = Index / Sides
		local P2 = (Index % Sides + 1) / Sides
		local A1 = P1 * Full
		local A2 = P2 * Full
		local Point1 = Base + Vector3.new(math.cos(A1), 0, math.sin(A1)) * Config.ChinaHatRadius
		local Point2 = Base + Vector3.new(math.cos(A2), 0, math.sin(A2)) * Config.ChinaHatRadius
		local S1 = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(Point1)
		local S2 = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(Point2)
		local Colour = HatColour(P1)

		Line.From = Vector2.new(S1.X, S1.Y)
		Line.To = Vector2.new(S2.X, S2.Y)
		Line.Color = Colour
		Line.Transparency = Config.ChinaHatLineTrans
		Line.Visible = true

		Triangle.PointA = TopPoint
		Triangle.PointB = Line.From
		Triangle.PointC = Line.To
		Triangle.Color = Colour
		Triangle.Transparency = Config.ChinaHatHatTrans
		Triangle.Visible = true
	end
end

local function ApplyHat()
	if not Config.ChinaHat then
		if #ChinaHatDraws > 0 then
			ClearHat()
		end
		return
	end
	BuildHat()
end

local SV_PARTS = {
	{ Name = "Torso", Size = Vector3.new(2, 2, 1), Offset = CFrame.new(0, 0, 0) },
	{ Name = "Head", Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1), Offset = CFrame.new(0, 1.5, 0) },
	{ Name = "LeftArm", Size = Vector3.new(1, 2, 1), Offset = CFrame.new(-1.5, 0, 0) },
	{ Name = "RightArm", Size = Vector3.new(1, 2, 1), Offset = CFrame.new(1.5, 0, 0) },
	{ Name = "LeftLeg", Size = Vector3.new(1, 2, 1), Offset = CFrame.new(-0.5, -2, 0) },
	{ Name = "RightLeg", Size = Vector3.new(1, 2, 1), Offset = CFrame.new(0.5, -2, 0) },
}

local SVModel = nil
local SVPieces = nil
local SVSmooth = nil

local function DestroyServerVis()
	if SVModel then
		pcall(function()
			SVModel:Destroy()
		end)
	end
	SVModel = nil
	SVPieces = nil
	SVSmooth = nil
end

local function CreateServerVis()
	DestroyServerVis()

	SVModel = Instance.new("Model")
	SVModel.Name = "FlameServerVis"
	SVPieces = {}

	local Material = Enum.Material[Config.ServerVisMaterial] or Enum.Material.ForceField

	for _, Def in ipairs(SV_PARTS) do
		local Part = Instance.new("Part")
		Part.Name = Def.Name
		Part.Size = Def.Size
		Part.Anchored = true
		Part.CanCollide = false
		Part.CanQuery = false
		Part.CanTouch = false
		Part.CastShadow = false
		Part.Material = Material
		Part.Color = Config.ServerVisColor
		Part.Transparency = Config.ServerVisTrans
		Part.Parent = SVModel

		local Adorn = Instance.new("BoxHandleAdornment")
		Adorn.Name = "SeeThrough"
		Adorn.Size = Def.Size + Vector3.new(0.04, 0.04, 0.04)
		Adorn.AlwaysOnTop = true
		Adorn.ZIndex = 0
		Adorn.Transparency = Config.ServerVisTrans
		Adorn.Color3 = Config.ServerVisColor
		Adorn.Adornee = Part
		Adorn.Parent = Part

		SVPieces[#SVPieces + 1] = { Part = Part, Offset = Def.Offset, Adorn = Adorn }
	end

	pcall(function()
		SVModel.Parent = Workspace
	end)
end

local function GetServerVisCFrame()
	local Supplied = getgenv().FlameServerCFrame or getgenv().ServerPosCFrame or getgenv().ServerVisCFrame

	if typeof(Supplied) == "CFrame" then
		return Supplied
	end

	local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
	local Root = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

	return Root and Root.CFrame or nil
end

local function StepServerVis(Dt)
	if not Config.ServerVis then
		if SVModel then
			DestroyServerVis()
		end
		return
	end

	local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
	local Root = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

	if not Root then
		if SVModel then
			DestroyServerVis()
		end
		return
	end

	if Config.ServerVisHideAttack and getgenv().FlameAttacking then
		if SVModel then
			DestroyServerVis()
		end
		return
	end

	local ClientCF = Root.CFrame
	local CF = GetServerVisCFrame() or ClientCF

	if Config.ServerVisOnlyDesync and (CF.Position - ClientCF.Position).Magnitude < 0.05 then
		if SVModel then
			DestroyServerVis()
		end
		return
	end

	if not SVModel then
		CreateServerVis()
	end

	if Config.ServerVisSmooth then
		if not SVSmooth then
			SVSmooth = CF
		end

		local Alpha = math.clamp((Dt or 1 / 60) * Config.ServerVisSmoothSpeed, 0, 1)
		SVSmooth = SVSmooth:Lerp(CF, Alpha)
		CF = SVSmooth
	else
		SVSmooth = CF
	end

	local Colour = Config.ServerVisColor
	local Alpha = Config.ServerVisTrans
	local Material = Enum.Material[Config.ServerVisMaterial] or Enum.Material.ForceField

	for _, Piece in ipairs(SVPieces) do
		Piece.Part.CFrame = CF * Piece.Offset
		Piece.Part.Color = Colour
		Piece.Part.Transparency = Alpha

		if Piece.Part.Material ~= Material then
			Piece.Part.Material = Material
		end

		if Piece.Adorn then
			Piece.Adorn.Color3 = Colour
			Piece.Adorn.Transparency = Alpha
		end
	end
end

local Api = {
	Skybox = ApplySkybox,
	Lighting = ApplyLighting,
	Colour = ApplyColour,
	Textures = ApplyTextures,
	Chams = ApplyChams,
	SelfChams = ApplyChams,
	ChinaHat = ApplyHat,
	ServerVis = StepServerVis,
	ClearChams = ClearChams,
	ClearSelfChams = ClearChams,
	ClearHat = ClearHat,
	SkyboxNames = OH_SKYBOX_NAMES,
	TextureNames = OH_TEXTURE_PACK_NAMES,
}

local Old = getgenv().FlameWorld
if Old then
	pcall(Old.ClearChams)
	pcall(Old.ClearHat)
end

if getgenv().FlameWorldLoop then
	getgenv().FlameWorldLoop:Disconnect()
end

if getgenv().FlameVisLoop then
	getgenv().FlameVisLoop:Disconnect()
end

if getgenv().FlameHatLoop then
	getgenv().FlameHatLoop:Disconnect()
end

getgenv().FlameWorld = Api

local Tick = 0

getgenv().FlameWorldLoop = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
	Tick = Tick + 1
	if Tick % 10 ~= 0 then
		return
	end
	ApplyChams()
	ApplyHat()
end)

getgenv().FlameVisLoop = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(StepServerVis)

getgenv().FlameHatLoop = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(RenderHat)

return Api
]==]
	local Chunk = loadstring(Source, "=FlameWorld")
	Source = nil
	if Chunk then
		local Ok, Result = pcall(Chunk, Config)
		if Ok and type(Result) == "table" then
			FlameWorld = Result
		end
	end
end

local FlameWeather = nil

do
	local Source = [==[
local Config = ...

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

local RAIN_POOL = 400
local SNOW_POOL = 300
local SPLASH_POOL = 120
local SPREAD = 100
local RAIN_TOP = 150
local SNOW_TOP = 150
local RAIN_SPEED = 35
local SNOW_SPEED = 20
local WIND_SPEED = 30
local RAIN_BASE_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(165, 183, 255)
local SNOW_BASE_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)

local World = nil
local RainFolder = nil
local SnowFolder = nil
local SplashFolder = nil
local RainDrops = {}
local SnowFlakes = {}
local SplashParts = {}
local SplashData = {}
local SplashIndex = 1
local RainSound = nil
local ThunderSound = nil
local OwnAtmo = nil
local OwnBloom = nil
local LoopConn = nil
local RayParams = RaycastParams.new()
local LightningTimer = 0
local NextLightning = 6
local Flashing = false

RayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude

local function GroundY(Position)
	RayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = { World, LocalPlayer.Character }
	local Result = Workspace:Raycast(Position + Vector3.new(0, 100, 0), Vector3.new(0, -500, 0), RayParams)
	if Result then
		return Result.Position.Y
	end
	return Position.Y - 3
end

local function MakePart(Parent, Shape, Size, Colour, Transparency)
	local Part = Instance.new("Part")
	Part.Shape = Shape
	Part.Size = Size
	Part.Color = Colour
	Part.Transparency = Transparency
	Part.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
	Part.Anchored = true
	Part.CanCollide = false
	Part.CanQuery = false
	Part.CanTouch = false
	Part.CastShadow = false
	Part.Parent = Parent
	return Part
end

local function Build()
	if World then
		return
	end

	World = Instance.new("Folder")
	World.Name = "FlameWeather"

	RainFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
	RainFolder.Name = "Rain"
	RainFolder.Parent = World

	SnowFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
	SnowFolder.Name = "Snow"
	SnowFolder.Parent = World

	SplashFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
	SplashFolder.Name = "Splashes"
	SplashFolder.Parent = World

	for Index = 1, RAIN_POOL do
		local Drop = MakePart(RainFolder, Enum.PartType.Cylinder, Vector3.new(1, 0.06, 0.06), RAIN_BASE_COLOR, 0.2)
		RainDrops[Index] = {
			Part = Drop,
			OffX = (math.random() - 0.5) * SPREAD,
			OffZ = (math.random() - 0.5) * SPREAD,
			Y = math.random(0, RAIN_TOP),
			Speed = 28 + math.random() * 20,
			Wind = -0.3 - math.random() * 0.5,
		}
	end

	for Index = 1, SNOW_POOL do
		local Flake = MakePart(SnowFolder, Enum.PartType.Ball, Vector3.new(0.25, 0.25, 0.25), SNOW_BASE_COLOR, 0.2)
		SnowFlakes[Index] = {
			Part = Flake,
			OffX = (math.random() - 0.5) * SPREAD,
			OffZ = (math.random() - 0.5) * SPREAD,
			Y = math.random(0, SNOW_TOP),
			Speed = 6 + math.random() * 8,
			Wind = -0.2 - math.random() * 0.6,
			Phase = math.random() * math.pi * 2,
		}
	end

	for Index = 1, SPLASH_POOL do
		SplashParts[Index] = MakePart(SplashFolder, Enum.PartType.Cylinder, Vector3.new(0.05, 0.6, 0.6), RAIN_BASE_COLOR, 1)
		SplashData[Index] = { Active = false, Life = 0, MaxLife = 0.35, X = 0, Z = 0, GroundY = 0 }
	end

	RainSound = Instance.new("Sound")
	RainSound.Name = "FlameRainSound"
	RainSound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://140364646624003"
	RainSound.Looped = true
	RainSound.Volume = 0.6

	ThunderSound = Instance.new("Sound")
	ThunderSound.Name = "FlameThunderSound"
	ThunderSound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://139319051979882"
	ThunderSound.Volume = 0.8

	local Camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
	if Camera then
		RainSound.Parent = Camera
		ThunderSound.Parent = Camera
	end

	OwnAtmo = Instance.new("Atmosphere")
	OwnAtmo.Name = "FlameAtmo"
	OwnAtmo.Parent = Lighting

	OwnBloom = Instance.new("BloomEffect")
	OwnBloom.Name = "FlameBloom"
	OwnBloom.Parent = Lighting

	World.Parent = Workspace
end

local function Clear()
	if LoopConn then
		LoopConn:Disconnect()
		LoopConn = nil
	end
	if World then
		pcall(function()
			World:Destroy()
		end)
	end
	if RainSound then
		pcall(function()
			RainSound:Stop()
			RainSound:Destroy()
		end)
	end
	if ThunderSound then
		pcall(function()
			ThunderSound:Stop()
			ThunderSound:Destroy()
		end)
	end
	if OwnAtmo then
		pcall(function()
			OwnAtmo:Destroy()
		end)
	end
	if OwnBloom then
		pcall(function()
			OwnBloom:Destroy()
		end)
	end
	World = nil
	RainFolder = nil
	SnowFolder = nil
	SplashFolder = nil
	RainDrops = {}
	SnowFlakes = {}
	SplashParts = {}
	SplashData = {}
	SplashIndex = 1
	RainSound = nil
	ThunderSound = nil
	OwnAtmo = nil
	OwnBloom = nil
	Flashing = false
end

local function DoLightning()
	if Flashing then
		return
	end
	Flashing = true

	if ThunderSound then
		pcall(function()
			ThunderSound:Play()
		end)
	end

	local OrigAmbient = Lighting.Ambient
	local OrigOutdoor = Lighting.OutdoorAmbient

	Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(120, 130, 210)
	Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(90, 100, 180)

	task.delay(0.1, function()
		if not World then
			Flashing = false
			return
		end
		Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(60, 68, 130)
		Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(40, 48, 100)

		task.delay(0.08, function()
			if not World then
				Flashing = false
				return
			end
			if math.random() > 0.45 then
				Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(100, 110, 195)
				Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(75, 85, 165)

				task.delay(0.09, function()
					if World then
						Lighting.Ambient = OrigAmbient
						Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = OrigOutdoor
					end
					Flashing = false
				end)
			else
				Lighting.Ambient = OrigAmbient
				Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = OrigOutdoor
				Flashing = false
			end
		end)
	end)
end

local function Step(Dt)
	local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
	local Root = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local Camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
	local Centre = Root and Root.Position or (Camera and Camera.CFrame.Position) or Vector3.zero
	local CentreX = Centre.X
	local CentreZ = Centre.Z

	local RainOn = Config.Rain
	local SnowOn = Config.Snow

	if RainOn or SnowOn then
		local Ground = GroundY(Centre)
		local Now = os.clock()

		if RainOn then
			local SpeedMul = Config.RainSpeed / RAIN_SPEED
			local WindMul = Config.RainWind / WIND_SPEED
			local Active = math.floor(RAIN_POOL * math.clamp(Config.RainDensity / 100, 0.05, 1))

			for Index = 1, RAIN_POOL do
				local Drop = RainDrops[Index]
				if Index > Active then
					if Drop.Part.Transparency ~= 1 then
						Drop.Part.Transparency = 1
					end
				else
					Drop.Y = Drop.Y - Drop.Speed * SpeedMul * Dt
					Drop.OffX = Drop.OffX + Drop.Wind * WindMul * Dt * 4

					if Drop.Y < Ground then
						if Config.RainSplash then
							local Data = SplashData[SplashIndex]
							Data.Active = true
							Data.Life = 0
							Data.MaxLife = 0.25 + math.random() * 0.3
							Data.X = CentreX + Drop.OffX
							Data.Z = CentreZ + Drop.OffZ
							Data.GroundY = Ground
							SplashIndex = SplashIndex % SPLASH_POOL + 1
						end

						Drop.Y = RAIN_TOP
						Drop.OffX = (math.random() - 0.5) * SPREAD
						Drop.OffZ = (math.random() - 0.5) * SPREAD
					end

					Drop.Part.CFrame = CFrame.new(CentreX + Drop.OffX, Drop.Y, CentreZ + Drop.OffZ)
						* CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.pi / 2 + Drop.Wind * WindMul * 0.15)
				end
			end

			for Index = 1, SPLASH_POOL do
				local Data = SplashData[Index]
				local Ring = SplashParts[Index]

				if Data.Active then
					Data.Life = Data.Life + Dt
					local Progress = Data.Life / Data.MaxLife
					local Scale = 1 + Progress * 5

					Ring.Size = Vector3.new(0.05, 0.6 * Scale, 0.6 * Scale)
					Ring.CFrame = CFrame.new(Data.X, Data.GroundY + 0.15, Data.Z) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.pi / 2)
					Ring.Transparency = math.min(0.1 + Progress * 0.9, 1)

					if Data.Life >= Data.MaxLife then
						Data.Active = false
						Ring.Transparency = 1
					end
				end
			end
		else
			for Index = 1, RAIN_POOL do
				if RainDrops[Index].Part.Transparency ~= 1 then
					RainDrops[Index].Part.Transparency = 1
				end
			end
			for Index = 1, SPLASH_POOL do
				if SplashParts[Index].Transparency ~= 1 then
					SplashParts[Index].Transparency = 1
				end
				SplashData[Index].Active = false
			end
		end

		if SnowOn then
			local SpeedMul = Config.SnowSpeed / SNOW_SPEED
			local WindMul = Config.SnowWind / WIND_SPEED
			local Active = math.floor(SNOW_POOL * math.clamp(Config.SnowDensity / 100, 0.05, 1))
			local Size = 0.1 + (Config.SnowSize / 100) * 0.5

			for Index = 1, SNOW_POOL do
				local Flake = SnowFlakes[Index]
				if Index > Active then
					if Flake.Part.Transparency ~= 1 then
						Flake.Part.Transparency = 1
					end
				else
					Flake.Y = Flake.Y - Flake.Speed * SpeedMul * Dt
					Flake.OffX = Flake.OffX + (Flake.Wind * WindMul + math.sin(Now * 1.5 + Flake.Phase) * 0.6) * Dt * 4

					if Flake.Y < Ground then
						Flake.Y = SNOW_TOP
						Flake.OffX = (math.random() - 0.5) * SPREAD
						Flake.OffZ = (math.random() - 0.5) * SPREAD
					end

					Flake.Part.Size = Vector3.new(Size, Size, Size)
					Flake.Part.CFrame = CFrame.new(CentreX + Flake.OffX, Flake.Y, CentreZ + Flake.OffZ)
				end
			end
		else
			for Index = 1, SNOW_POOL do
				if SnowFlakes[Index].Part.Transparency ~= 1 then
					SnowFlakes[Index].Part.Transparency = 1
				end
			end
		end
	end

	if Config.WeatherLightning and (RainOn or SnowOn) then
		local Rate = Config.LightningRate / 50
		LightningTimer = LightningTimer + Dt
		if LightningTimer >= NextLightning * (1 / Rate) then
			DoLightning()
			LightningTimer = 0
			NextLightning = 4 + math.random() * 8
		end
	end
end

local function EnsureLoop()
	local Wanted = (Config.Rain or Config.Snow) and true or false

	if Wanted and not LoopConn then
		LoopConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(Step)
	elseif not Wanted and LoopConn then
		LoopConn:Disconnect()
		LoopConn = nil
	end
end

local function Apply()
	if not (Config.Rain or Config.Snow or Config.WeatherAtmo or Config.WeatherBloom or Config.WeatherLightning) then
		if World then
			Clear()
		end
		return
	end

	Build()

	local RainOn = Config.Rain
	local SnowOn = Config.Snow
	local RainColour = Config.RainColor
	local RainAlpha = Config.RainOpacity / 100

	RainFolder.Parent = RainOn and World or nil
	SnowFolder.Parent = SnowOn and World or nil
	SplashFolder.Parent = (RainOn and Config.RainSplash) and World or nil

	for Index = 1, RAIN_POOL do
		RainDrops[Index].Part.Color = RainColour
		RainDrops[Index].Part.Transparency = RainAlpha
	end

	local SnowColour = Config.SnowColor
	local SnowAlpha = Config.SnowOpacity / 100

	for Index = 1, SNOW_POOL do
		SnowFlakes[Index].Part.Color = SnowColour
		SnowFlakes[Index].Part.Transparency = SnowAlpha
	end

	for Index = 1, SPLASH_POOL do
		SplashParts[Index].Color = RainColour
	end

	if RainSound then
		if RainOn then
			RainSound.Volume = Config.RainVolume / 100
			if not RainSound.Playing then
				pcall(function()
					RainSound:Play()
				end)
			end
		else
			pcall(function()
				RainSound:Stop()
			end)
		end
	end

	if ThunderSound then
		ThunderSound.Volume = Config.ThunderVolume / 100
	end

	if OwnAtmo then
		if Config.WeatherAtmo then
			OwnAtmo.Density = Config.AtmoDensity / 100
			OwnAtmo.Offset = 0
			OwnAtmo.Color = Config.AtmoColor
			OwnAtmo.Decay = Color3.fromRGB(100, 120, 200)
			OwnAtmo.Glare = 0.3
			OwnAtmo.Haze = Config.AtmoHaze / 50
		else
			OwnAtmo.Density = 0
			OwnAtmo.Haze = 0
		end
	end

	if OwnBloom then
		OwnBloom.Enabled = Config.WeatherBloom
		OwnBloom.Intensity = Config.BloomIntensity / 100
		OwnBloom.Size = Config.BloomSize
		OwnBloom.Threshold = 1.2
	end

	EnsureLoop()
end

local Old = getgenv().FlameWeather
if Old then
	pcall(Old.Clear)
end

local Api = {
	Update = Apply,
	Clear = Clear,
}

getgenv().FlameWeather = Api

return Api
]==]
	local Chunk = loadstring(Source, "=FlameWeather")
	Source = nil
	if Chunk then
		local Ok, Result = pcall(Chunk, Config)
		if Ok and type(Result) == "table" then
			FlameWeather = Result
		end
	end
end

local Table = ESP['Table'];
local Cache, Threads = {}, {};

local Fonts = {}; do
    local function FontsRegister(Name, Weight, Style, Asset)
        if isfile(Name .. ".font") then
            local Ok, Cached = pcall(getcustomasset, Name .. ".font")
            if Ok and Cached then
                return Cached
            end
            pcall(delfile, Name .. ".font")
        end

        if not isfile(Asset.Id) then
            writefile(Asset.Id, Asset.Font)
        end

        local Info = {
            name = Name,
            faces = {
                {
                    name = "Normal",
                    weight = Weight,
                    style = Style,
                    assetId = getcustomasset(Asset.Id),
                },
            },
        }

        writefile(Name .. ".font", HttpService:JSONEncode(Info))
        return getcustomasset(Name .. ".font")
    end;

    pcall(function()
        Fonts.Tahoma = FontsRegister("Tahoma", 400, "Normal", {
            Id = "Tahoma.ttf",
            Font = game:HttpGet("https://github.com/i77lhm/storage/raw/refs/heads/main/fonts/fs-tahoma-8px.ttf"),
        })
    end)

    pcall(function()
        Fonts.XPTahoma = FontsRegister("XPTahoma", 400, "Normal", {
            Id = "Tahoma8PTBOLD.ttf",
            Font = game:HttpGet("https://github.com/sametexe001/luas/raw/refs/heads/main/fonts/TAHOMA-8PT-BOLD-WINDOWS-XP.TTF"),
        })
    end)

    pcall(function()
        Fonts.SmallestPixel = FontsRegister("SmallestPixel", 400, "Normal", {
            Id = "smallest_pixel-7.ttf",
            Font = game:HttpGet("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sametexe001/luas/main/smallest_pixel-7.ttf")
        })
    end)

    pcall(function()
        Fonts.ProggyTiny = FontsRegister("ProggyTiny", 400, "Normal", {
            Id = "ProggyTinyyyy.ttf",
            Font = game:HttpGet("https://github.com/i77lhm/storage/raw/refs/heads/main/fonts/ProggyTiny.ttf")
        })
    end)

    pcall(function()
        Fonts.ProggyClean = FontsRegister("ProggyClean", 400, "Normal", {
            Id = "ProggyClean.ttf",
            Font = game:HttpGet("https://github.com/i77lhm/storage/raw/main/fonts/ProggyClean.ttf"),
        })
    end)

    local _espFallbackFont = nil
    pcall(function() _espFallbackFont = Font.fromEnum(Enum.Font.Code) end)
    if not _espFallbackFont then
        pcall(function() _espFallbackFont = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/RobotoMono.json", Enum.FontWeight.Regular, Enum.FontStyle.Normal) end)
    end

    local function MkFont(id)
        if id then
            local ok, f = pcall(Font.new, id, Enum.FontWeight.Regular, Enum.FontStyle.Normal)
            if ok and f then return f end
        end
        return _espFallbackFont
    end

    ESP.ProggyTiny = MkFont(Fonts.ProggyClean);
    ESP.TahomaBold = MkFont(Fonts.XPTahoma);
    ESP.ProggyClean = MkFont(Fonts.ProggyClean);
    ESP.Tahoma = MkFont(Fonts.Tahoma);
    ESP.SmallestPixel = MkFont(Fonts.SmallestPixel);
end

ESP.__index = ESP;

function ESP:CreateObjects(Name, Prop)
    local New = Instance.new(Name);

    for Property, Value in Prop or {} do
        New[Property] = Value;
    end;
            
    return New;
end

function ESP:CreateThreads(Name, Signal, Callback)
    local Connection = Signal:Connect(Callback);
    Threads[Name] = Connection;
    return Connection;
end

ESP.Holder = ESP:CreateObjects("ScreenGui", {
    Name = "\n",
    Parent = gethui(),
    ScreenInsets = Enum.ScreenInsets.DeviceSafeInsets,
    ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Global,
    ResetOnSpawn = false,
    DisplayOrder = 10000,
    IgnoreGuiInset = true,
})
