Window:InitSettings()

task.spawn(function()
	ScanShop()
	UpdateFriends()
	local Char = LocalPlayer.Character
	if Char and (Char:FindFirstChild("FORCEFIELD") or Char:FindFirstChild("ForceField")) then
		SpawnBuyUntil = os.clock() + 12
		task.spawn(function()
			while Char.Parent and (Char:FindFirstChild("FORCEFIELD") or Char:FindFirstChild("ForceField")) do task.wait(0.2) end
			SpawnBuyUntil = 0
			SpawnBuyingDone = true
		end)
	end
end)

if getgenv().FlameRageLoop then
	getgenv().FlameRageLoop:Disconnect()
end

local AntiStompTick = 0

local function FindStomper(Root)
	for _, Player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if Player ~= LocalPlayer then
			local Character = Player.Character
			local TheirRoot = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if TheirRoot then
				local Offset = TheirRoot.Position - Root.Position
				if Offset.Y > 2.5 and Offset.Y < 14 then
					local Flat = Vector3.new(Offset.X, 0, Offset.Z)
					if Flat.Magnitude < 4.5 then
						return TheirRoot.Position
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	return nil
end

getgenv().FlameRageLoop = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
	ServerCFrame = nil
	ServerVelocity = nil
	Config.SpectatedThisFrame = nil;

	if Config.NoClip and LocalPlayer.Character then
		ApplyNoClip(LocalPlayer.Character)
	end

	if Config.AntiSit and LocalPlayer.Character then
		ApplyAntiSit(LocalPlayer.Character)
		StartSeatCleaner()
		UnseatSelf(LocalPlayer.Character)
	end

	EnsureLoadout()

	if Config.AntiStomp and LocalPlayer.Character and IsKnocked(LocalPlayer.Character) then
		if not Config.AntiStompFlung then
			Config.AntiStompFlung = true
			local FlingRoot = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if FlingRoot then
				Config.AntiStompPos = FlingRoot.CFrame
				pcall(function()
					setnetworkowner(FlingRoot, true)
				end)
				FlingRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -2147483647, 0)
				FlingRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(65536, 65534, 65536)
			end
			local FlingHumanoid = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if FlingHumanoid then
				task.spawn(function()
					for _ = 1, 10 do
						if not FlingHumanoid.Parent then
							break
						end
						FlingHumanoid.Health = 0
						RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
					end
				end)
			end
		end
	elseif Config.AntiStompFlung then
		Config.AntiStompFlung = false
	end

	if Config.AntiFling and LocalPlayer.Character then
		local FlingRoot = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if FlingRoot then
			if Config.AntiFlingChar ~= LocalPlayer.Character then
				Config.AntiFlingChar = LocalPlayer.Character
				Config.AntiFlingPos = nil
			end

			if InVoid or BuyBusy or Following or Stomping or HoldMode == "void" then
				Config.AntiFlingPos = nil
			else
				local Position = FlingRoot.Position
				local Last = Config.AntiFlingPos

				if Last and (Position - Last).Magnitude > 60 then
					FlingRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(Last, Last + FlingRoot.CFrame.LookVector)
					FlingRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
					Config.AntiFlingPos = Last
				else
					Config.AntiFlingPos = Position
				end
			end
		end
	end

	if not Config.Enabled and not Config.KnifeEnabled then
		Config.BotTarget = nil
	end

	if Config.TargetHUD then
		if getgenv().LilacTargetHUD == nil and getgenv().LilacTargetHUDSource then
			pcall(function()
				loadstring(getgenv().LilacTargetHUDSource)()
			end)
			local Hud = getgenv().LilacTargetHUD
			if Hud then
				Hud.AutoTarget = false
			end
			Config.HUDTarget = nil
		end

		local Hud = getgenv().LilacTargetHUD
		if Hud then
			local Wanted = Config.BotTarget or Config.TargetAimCurrent
			if Wanted ~= Config.HUDTarget then
				Config.HUDTarget = Wanted
				if Wanted then
					pcall(function()
						Hud:SetTarget(Wanted)
					end)
				else
					pcall(function()
						Hud:ClearTarget()
					end)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	Config.TargetAimCurrent = nil
	if Config.TargetAim then
		local Picked = getgenv().FlameTargetAimPickCurrent and getgenv().FlameTargetAimPickCurrent()
		if Picked then
			Config.TargetAimCurrent = Picked
		end
	end

	local AimPlayer = Config.TargetAimCurrent or (Config.TargetAim and Config.BotTarget) or nil
	local AimCharacter = AimPlayer and AimPlayer.Character

	if AimCharacter and AimCharacter.Parent then
		local Highlight = getgenv().FlameTargetAimHighlight
		if not Highlight or Highlight.Parent == nil then
			pcall(function()
				Highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
				Highlight.FillTransparency = 0.65
				Highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0
				Highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
				Highlight.Parent = gethui and gethui() or game:GetService("CoreGui")
			end)
			getgenv().FlameTargetAimHighlight = Highlight
		end
		if Highlight then
			Highlight.Adornee = AimCharacter
			Highlight.FillColor = Config.TargetAimHighlightColor
			Highlight.OutlineColor = Config.TargetAimHighlightOutline
			Highlight.Enabled = Config.TargetAimHighlight
			if Highlight.Enabled and Highlight.Parent == nil then
				Highlight.Parent = gethui and gethui() or game:GetService("CoreGui")
			end
		end
	elseif getgenv().FlameTargetAimHighlight then
		getgenv().FlameTargetAimHighlight.Adornee = nil
	end


	if not Config.Enabled and not Config.KnifeEnabled and AimPlayer then
		local MyCharacter = LocalPlayer.Character
		local MyRoot = MyCharacter and MyCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local TheirRoot = AimCharacter and AimCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if MyRoot and TheirRoot then
			local TheirPos = TheirRoot.Position
			local MyPos = MyRoot.Position

			if Config.TargetAimStrafe then
				local Radius = Config.TargetAimStrafeRadius

				if Config.TargetAimStrafeMode == "strafe" then
					local Angle = (os.clock() * 2) % 1 * 6.2831853071796
					local Point = Vector3.new(TheirPos.X + math.sin(Angle) * Radius, MyPos.Y, TheirPos.Z + math.cos(Angle) * Radius)
					ServerCFrame = CFrame.new(Point, Vector3.new(TheirPos.X, MyPos.Y, TheirPos.Z))
					ServerVelocity = Vector3.zero
				elseif Config.TargetAimStrafeMode == "random" then
					local Point = Vector3.new(TheirPos.X + math.random(-Radius, Radius), MyPos.Y, TheirPos.Z + math.random(-Radius, Radius))
					ServerCFrame = CFrame.new(Point, Vector3.new(TheirPos.X, MyPos.Y, TheirPos.Z))
					ServerVelocity = Vector3.zero
				else
					if math.random(1, 3) == 1 then
						ServerCFrame = CFrame.new(math.random(-2147483647, 2147483647), math.random(-400, 2147483647), math.random(-2147483647, 2147483647))
						ServerVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -100, 0)
					else
						local Point = Vector3.new(TheirPos.X + math.random(-Radius, Radius), MyPos.Y, TheirPos.Z + math.random(-Radius, Radius))
						ServerCFrame = CFrame.new(Point, Vector3.new(TheirPos.X, MyPos.Y, TheirPos.Z))
						ServerVelocity = Vector3.zero
					end
				end
			elseif Config.TargetAimFaceTarget then
				ServerCFrame = CFrame.new(MyPos, Vector3.new(TheirPos.X, MyPos.Y, TheirPos.Z))
				ServerVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end
		end
	end

	(function()

	if Config.KnifeEnabled then
		local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
		local Humanoid = Character and Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local Root = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not Root or not Humanoid or Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
			return
		end

		if Config.NoClip and Character then
			ApplyNoClip(Character)
		end

		if Config.AntiSit and Character then
			ApplyAntiSit(Character)
			StartSeatCleaner()
			UnseatSelf(Character)
		end

		if Root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude < 0.1 then
			Root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -0.1, 0)
		end

		if BuyBusy then
			ServerCFrame = nil
			ServerVelocity = nil
			Unglue()
			return
		end

		if not Character:FindFirstChild("[Knife]") then
			EquipKnife()
		end

		local Tool = Character:FindFirstChild("[Knife]")
		if not Tool then
			if not BuyBusy and GetCash() >= 11000 then
				BuyItem("knife", 10, function()
					return HasKnife() ~= nil
				end, HasKnife, false, 30, true, Config.BuyClickDelay)
			end
			if not BuyBusy then
				StartVoid(Root)
				InVoid = true
				HoldVoid(Root)
			end
			return
		end

		local Target = FindKnifeTarget(Root)
		if not Target then
			Config.BotTarget = nil
			Following = false
			Stomping = false
			Unglue()
			StartVoid(Root)
			InVoid = true
			HoldVoid(Root)
			return
		end

		Config.BotTarget = Target

		local Torso = Target.Character:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso") or Target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not Torso then
			Following = false
			Stomping = false
			Unglue()
			StartVoid(Root)
			InVoid = true
			HoldVoid(Root)
			return
		end

		local Dead = IsDead(Target.Character)
		local Knocked = IsKnocked(Target.Character)

		if Dead then
			Following = false
			Stomping = false
			Unglue()
			StartVoid(Root)
			InVoid = true
			HoldVoid(Root)
			return
		end

		if Config.KnifeSpectate then
			Config.SpectatedThisFrame = Target
		end

		if Knocked then
			Following = false
			Stomping = true
			InVoid = false
			HoldMode = "attack"
			StompVictim = Target
			pcall(function()
				Tool:Deactivate()
			end)
			getgenv().FlameDoStomp(Target.Character)
		else
			if Config.KnifeCustomTiming then
				local Now = os.clock()
				if Config.KnifePhaseStart == nil then
					Config.KnifePhaseStart = Now
					Config.KnifePhaseAttack = true
				end
				local Duration = Config.KnifePhaseAttack and Config.KnifeAttackTime or Config.KnifeHideTime
				if Now - Config.KnifePhaseStart >= Duration then
					Config.KnifePhaseAttack = not Config.KnifePhaseAttack
					Config.KnifePhaseStart = Now
				end
				if not Config.KnifePhaseAttack then
					Following = false
					Stomping = false
					Unglue()
					StartVoid(Root)
					InVoid = true
					HoldVoid(Root)
					return
				end
			end
			Following = true
			Stomping = false
			InVoid = false
			HoldMode = "attack"
			local TargetRoot = Target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or Torso
			pcall(function()
				sethiddenproperty(Root, "PhysicsRepRootRef", InstanceHandle.new(TargetRoot))
			end)
			ServerCFrame = CFrame.new(getgenv().FlameKnifeReachPosition(TargetRoot), TargetRoot.Position)
			ServerVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -100, 0)
			if os.clock() - LastFire > (Config.KnifeCustomTiming and Config.FireInterval or Config.KnifeAttackTime) then
				local AttackFlag = Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
				AttackFlag = AttackFlag and AttackFlag:FindFirstChild("Attacking")
				if not (AttackFlag and AttackFlag.Value) and not Character:GetAttribute("Attacking") then
					LastFire = os.clock()
					task.spawn(function()
						pcall(function()
							Tool:Activate()
						end)
						RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
						pcall(function()
							Tool:Deactivate()
						end)
					end)
				end
			end
		end
		return
	end


	if not Config.Enabled then
		InVoid = false
		Following = false
		Stomping = false
		Unglue()
		ReleaseVoid()
		RestoreVoidSurvivable()
		if NoClipConnection then
			NoClipConnection:Disconnect()
			NoClipConnection = nil
		end
		return
	end

	local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
	local Humanoid = Character and Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	local LocalKnocked = IsKnocked(Character)
	local LocalDead = Humanoid == nil or Humanoid.Health <= 0 or IsDead(Character)

	if Config.NoClip and Character then
		ApplyNoClip(Character)
	end

	if Config.AntiSit and Character then
		ApplyAntiSit(Character)
		StartSeatCleaner()
		UnseatSelf(Character)
	end

	local WasLocalDead = LastLocalDead
	LastLocalDead = LocalDead
	if LocalDead and not WasLocalDead then
		local Killer = nil
		if Character then
			local Effects = Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
			local LD = Effects and Effects:FindFirstChild("LastDamager")
			local Found = PlayerFromValue(LD and LD.Value)
			if Found and Found ~= LocalPlayer then
				Killer = Found
			end
		end
		if not Killer and LastAttacker and os.clock() - LastAttackerTime < 15 then
			Killer = LastAttacker
		end
		if Killer and Config.ScoreTrack then
			Credit(Killer, false)
		end
		StompVictim = nil
		RevengeTarget = nil
		LastAttacker = nil
	end
	local LocalKnocked = Character and IsKnocked(Character)
	if LocalKnocked and not Config.WasLocalKnocked and Config.ScoreTrack then
		local Effects = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
		local Damager = Effects and Effects:FindFirstChild("LastDamager")
		local Attacker = PlayerFromValue(Damager and Damager.Value)
		if Attacker and Attacker ~= LocalPlayer then
			Credit(Attacker, false)
		end
	end
	Config.WasLocalKnocked = LocalKnocked

	if LocalDead then
		HoldMode = nil
		Following = false
		Stomping = false
		InVoid = false
		Unglue()
		return
	end

	if StompVictim then
		local VictimCharacter = StompVictim.Character
		if not StompVictim.Parent or not VictimCharacter then
			StompVictim = nil
		elseif DeadFlag(VictimCharacter) then
			if LockedTarget == StompVictim then
				LockedTarget = nil
			end
		elseif not IsKnocked(VictimCharacter) then
			StompVictim = nil
		end
	end

	if Config.ScoreTrack then
		Config.KillScanTick = (Config.KillScanTick or 0) + 1
		if Config.KillScanTick % 12 == 0 then
			for _, Player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				if Player ~= LocalPlayer then
					local TheirCharacter = Player.Character
					if TheirCharacter then
						local TheirHumanoid = TheirCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
						local TheirEffects = TheirCharacter:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
						local LastDamager = TheirEffects and TheirEffects:FindFirstChild("LastDamager")
						local Killer = PlayerFromValue(LastDamager and LastDamager.Value)

						local KnockedFlag = TheirEffects and TheirEffects:FindFirstChild("K.O")
						local IsKnockedNow = KnockedFlag and KnockedFlag.Value == true

						if TheirHumanoid and (TheirHumanoid.Health <= 0 or DeadFlag(TheirCharacter)) then
							if Killer == LocalPlayer and Credited[Player] ~= TheirCharacter then
								Credited[Player] = TheirCharacter
								LastCredit[Player] = os.clock()
								Credit(Player, true)
							end
						else
							Credited[Player] = nil
						end

						if IsKnockedNow and Killer == LocalPlayer and Config.KnockCredited[Player] ~= TheirCharacter then
							Config.KnockCredited[Player] = TheirCharacter
							Credit(Player, true)
						end
						if not IsKnockedNow then
							Config.KnockCredited[Player] = nil
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local Root = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not Root then
		return
	end
	TickCFrame = Root.CFrame
	TickVelocity = Root.AssemblyLinearVelocity

	if BuyBusy then
		Following = false
		Stomping = false
		InVoid = true
		HoldMode = nil
		ServerCFrame = nil
		ServerVelocity = nil
		Unglue()
		local Held = Character:FindFirstChild("[Flamethrower]")
		if Held then
			pcall(function()
				Held:Deactivate()
			end)
		end
		return
	end

	if HoldMode == "void" then
		HoldVoid(Root)
	end
	
	if Humanoid and Humanoid.Health > 0 then
		local CurrentHealth = Humanoid.Health
		if Character:GetAttribute("LastHealth") then
			local LastHealth = Character:GetAttribute("LastHealth")
			if CurrentHealth < LastHealth then
				local Effects = Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
				local LD = Effects and Effects:FindFirstChild("LastDamager")
				local Attacker = PlayerFromValue(LD and LD.Value)
				if Attacker and Attacker ~= LocalPlayer then
					LastAttacker = Attacker
					LastAttackerTime = os.clock()
					if Config.LPNotif then
						Library:Notification({ Name = "hit by " .. PlayerTag(Attacker) .. "  -" .. math.floor((LastHealth - CurrentHealth) + 0.5), Time = Config.HitNotifDuration })
					end
					if not StompVictim then
						RevengeTarget = Attacker
					end
				end
			end
		end
		Character:SetAttribute("LastHealth", CurrentHealth)
	end

	if Root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude < 0.1 then
		Root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -0.1, 0)
	end

	if Character ~= LastCharacter then
		LastCharacter = Character
		SpawnGate = os.clock()
	end

	local Tool = EquipFlamethrower()

	local Target = FindTarget()

	if not Target or not Tool then
		Config.BotTarget = nil
		Following = false
		Stomping = false
		Unglue()
		if Tool then
			pcall(function()
				Tool:Deactivate()
			end)
		end
		StartVoid(Root)
		InVoid = true
		HoldVoid(Root)
		return
	end

	Config.BotTarget = Target

	local Torso = Target.Character:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso") or Target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not Torso then
		Following = false
		Stomping = false
		Unglue()
		StartVoid(Root)
		InVoid = true
		HoldVoid(Root)
		return
	end

	local Ready = LocalReady(Character)
	if not Ready then
		pcall(function()
			Tool:Deactivate()
		end)
		if IsKnocked(Character) then
			Following = false
			Stomping = false
			Unglue()
			StartVoid(Root)
			InVoid = true
			HoldVoid(Root)
			return
		end
	end

	if Config.Spectate then
		Config.SpectatedThisFrame = Target
	end

	if Config.AntiConnection then
		local Now = os.clock()
		if Now - VoidTimer > Config.HideTime then
			VoidTimer = Now
			local Point = VoidPoint or VoidCFrame().Position
			for _, Player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				if Player ~= LocalPlayer and Player.Character then
					local Other = Player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					if Other and (Other.Position - Point).Magnitude < 250 then
						VoidPoint = VoidCFrame().Position
						break
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local Knocked = IsKnocked(Target.Character)
	local Dead = IsDead(Target.Character)

	if Target ~= LastTarget then
		LastTarget = Target
	end

	if Dead then
		if LockedTarget == Target then
			LockedTarget = nil
		end
		Following = false
		Stomping = false
		Unglue()
		StartVoid(Root)
		InVoid = true
		HoldVoid(Root)
		pcall(function()
			Tool:Deactivate()
		end)
		if RevengeTarget == Target then
			RevengeTarget = nil
		end
		return
	end

	if Knocked and not Dead then
		Following = false
		Stomping = true
		InVoid = false
		StompVictim = Target
		pcall(function()
			Tool:Deactivate()
		end)
		EnsureVoidSurvivable()
		getgenv().FlameDoStomp(Target.Character)
		return
	end

	Stomping = false

	local Ammo = Tool:FindFirstChild("Ammo")

	if Ammo and Ammo.Value <= 0 then
		Following = false
		Stomping = false
		Unglue()
		StartVoid(Root)
		InVoid = true
		HoldVoid(Root)
		if ServerCFrame then
			ServerCFrame = CFrame.new(ServerCFrame.Position, Vector3.new(Torso.Position.X, ServerCFrame.Position.Y, Torso.Position.Z))
		end
		Reload(Tool)
		return
	end

	if not CanDamage(Target.Character) then
		Following = false
		Stomping = false
		Unglue()
		StartVoid(Root)
		InVoid = true
		HoldVoid(Root)
		return
	end

	if Config.CustomTiming then
		local Now = os.clock()
		if Config.PhaseStart == nil then
			Config.PhaseStart = Now
			Config.PhaseAttack = true
		end
		local Duration = Config.PhaseAttack and Config.AttackTime or Config.HideTime
		if Now - Config.PhaseStart >= Duration then
			Config.PhaseAttack = not Config.PhaseAttack
			Config.PhaseStart = Now
		end
		if not Config.PhaseAttack then
			Following = false
			Stomping = false
			Unglue()
			StartVoid(Root)
			InVoid = true
			HoldVoid(Root)
			return
		end
	end

	Glue(Target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or Torso)

	Following = true
	Stomping = false
	InVoid = false
	HoldMode = "attack"
	HitClaim[Target] = os.clock()
	local AimPosition = Torso.Position
	local Chest = Target.Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
	if Chest then
		AimPosition = (Torso.Position + Chest.Position) / 2
	end
	local LookPoint = AimPosition
	LastHoldFrame = CFrame.new(StrafePosition(AimPosition), LookPoint)
	ServerCFrame = LastHoldFrame
	ServerVelocity = VoidPush

	if Config.AntiStomp then
		AntiStompTick = AntiStompTick + 1
		if AntiStompTick % 3 == 0 then
			local Stomper = FindStomper(Root)
			if Stomper then
				local serverPos = ServerCFrame and ServerCFrame.Position or Root.Position
				local Away = Vector3.new(serverPos.X - Stomper.X, 0, serverPos.Z - Stomper.Z)
				if Away.Magnitude < 0.1 then
					Away = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
				end
				Away = Away.Unit
				ServerCFrame = CFrame.new(serverPos + Away * 7 + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0))
				ServerVelocity = Away * 95 + Vector3.new(0, 18, 0)
			end
		end
	end

	if Ready and Ammo and Ammo.Value > 0 and not IsReloading() then
		if os.clock() - LastFire > (Config.CustomTiming and Config.FireInterval or Config.AttackTime) then
			LastFire = os.clock()
			Config.TracerUntil = os.clock() + 0.12
			ShotFired[Target] = os.clock()
			ShotHit[Target] = false
			pcall(function()
				Tool:Activate()
			end)
		end
	else
		Reload(Tool)
	end
	end)()

	if Config.VoidHideActive and not Config.Enabled and not Config.KnifeEnabled and LocalPlayer.Character then
		local VoidRoot = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if VoidRoot then
			StartVoid(VoidRoot)
			InVoid = true
			HoldVoid(VoidRoot)
		end
	end
	getgenv().FlameServerCFrame = ServerCFrame

	local DesyncRoot = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if DesyncRoot and ServerCFrame then
		local oldCFrame = DesyncRoot.CFrame
		local oldVelocity = DesyncRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity
		DesyncRoot.CFrame = ServerCFrame
		DesyncRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = ServerVelocity or Vector3.new(0, -100, 0)
		RunService:BindToRenderStep("FlameClientSync", 101, function()
			DesyncRoot.CFrame = oldCFrame
			DesyncRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = oldVelocity
			RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("FlameClientSync")
		end)
	end

	if Config.SpectatedThisFrame then
		local SpectateCharacter = Config.SpectatedThisFrame.Character
		local SpectateHead = SpectateCharacter and SpectateCharacter:FindFirstChild("Head")
		if SpectateHead then
			Camera.CameraSubject = SpectateHead
		end
		getgenv().FlameSpectateTarget = Config.SpectatedThisFrame
	elseif getgenv().FlameSpectateTarget then
		local MyCharacter = LocalPlayer.Character
		local MyHumanoid = MyCharacter and MyCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if MyHumanoid and Camera.CameraSubject ~= MyHumanoid then
			Camera.CameraSubject = MyHumanoid
		end
		getgenv().FlameSpectateTarget = nil
	end
end)

Library:Notification({ Name = "Avenir Rage Loaded Pussy", Time = 4 })

;(function()
local StateText = Drawing.new("Text")
local StateShadow = Drawing.new("Text")
getgenv().FlameStateText = StateText
getgenv().FlameStateShadow = StateShadow
StateText.Visible = false
StateShadow.Visible = false
StateText.Center = true
StateShadow.Center = true
StateText.Outline = true
StateShadow.Outline = false
StateText.Size = 14
StateShadow.Size = 14
StateText.Font = 2
StateShadow.Font = 2
StateText.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
StateShadow.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
StateText.ZIndex = 2
StateShadow.ZIndex = 1

getgenv().FlameTracer = Drawing.new("Line")
getgenv().FlameTracer.Visible = false
getgenv().FlameTracer.Thickness = Config.TracerThickness
getgenv().FlameTracer.Transparency = 0
getgenv().FlameTracer.Color = Config.TracerColor

if getgenv().FlameTracerBullets then
	pcall(function()
		getgenv().FlameTracerBullets:Disconnect()
	end)
	getgenv().FlameTracerBullets = nil
end
getgenv().FlameTracerBulletPath = nil

if getgenv().FlameTracerWatch then
	task.cancel(getgenv().FlameTracerWatch)
	getgenv().FlameTracerWatch = nil
end

do
	local function OnBullet(Object)
		if Object.Name ~= "BULLET_RAYS" then
			return
		end

		local Beam = Object:FindFirstChild("NewGunBeam") or Object:FindFirstChild("GunBeam")
		if not Beam then
			Beam = Object:WaitForChild("NewGunBeam", 0.5) or Object:WaitForChild("GunBeam", 0.5)
		end
		local Attachment = Beam and Beam.Attachment1
		if not Attachment then
			return
		end

		Config.TracerStart = Object.Position
		Config.TracerEnd = Attachment.WorldPosition
		Config.TracerUntil = os.clock() + 0.15
	end

	getgenv().FlameTracerWatch = task.spawn(function()
		while true do
			local Ignored = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Ignored")
			local Siren = Ignored and Ignored:FindFirstChild("Siren")
			local BulletPath = Siren and Siren:FindFirstChild("Radius")
			if BulletPath and BulletPath ~= getgenv().FlameTracerBulletPath then
				if getgenv().FlameTracerBullets then
					pcall(function()
						getgenv().FlameTracerBullets:Disconnect()
					end)
				end
				getgenv().FlameTracerBulletPath = BulletPath
				getgenv().FlameTracerBullets = BulletPath.ChildAdded:Connect(OnBullet)
			end
			task.wait(1)
		end
	end)
end

if getgenv().FlameTracerInput then
	getgenv().FlameTracerInput:Disconnect()
	getgenv().FlameTracerInput = nil
end

getgenv().FlameTracerInput = game:GetService("UserInputService").InputBegan:Connect(function(Input)
	if getgenv().FlameTracerBullets then
		return
	end
	if Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
		local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
		if Character and Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") then
			Config.TracerUntil = os.clock() + 0.12
		end
	end
end)

RunService:BindToRenderStep("FlameTracer", 99, function()
	local TracerLine = getgenv().FlameTracer
	if not Config.Tracer or os.clock() > Config.TracerUntil then
		if TracerLine.Visible then
			TracerLine.Visible = false
		end
		Config.TracerStart = nil
		Config.TracerEnd = nil
		return
	end

	local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
	local Root = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not Root then
		TracerLine.Visible = false
		return
	end

	local Origin
	local Aim

	if Config.TracerStart and Config.TracerEnd then
		Origin = Config.TracerStart
		Aim = Config.TracerEnd
	else
		local Tool = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
		local Handle = Tool and Tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
		Origin = Handle and Handle.Position or Root.Position

		local Target = LastTarget
		local TargetCharacter = Target and Target.Character
		local Hit = TargetCharacter and (TargetCharacter:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or TargetCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
		if Hit then
			Aim = Hit.Position
		else
			local MousePos = game:GetService("UserInputService"):GetMouseLocation()
			local Ray = Camera:ViewportPointToRay(MousePos.X, MousePos.Y)
			Aim = Ray.Origin + Ray.Direction * 500
		end
	end

	local From, FromOn = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(Origin)
	local To, ToOn = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(Aim)
	if not FromOn or not ToOn then
		TracerLine.Visible = false
		return
	end

	TracerLine.From = Vector2.new(From.X, From.Y)
	TracerLine.To = Vector2.new(To.X, To.Y)
	TracerLine.Thickness = Config.TracerThickness
	TracerLine.Color = Config.TracerColor
	TracerLine.Visible = true
end)

LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(Character)
	do
		SpawnBuyUntil = 0
		SpawnBuyingDone = false
		local Char = Character or LocalPlayer.Character
		if Char then
			task.spawn(function()
				for _ = 1, 60 do
					if not Char.Parent then break end
					if Char:FindFirstChild("FORCEFIELD") or Char:FindFirstChild("ForceField") then SpawnBuyUntil = os.clock() + 12; break end
					task.wait(0.1)
				end
				while Char.Parent and (Char:FindFirstChild("FORCEFIELD") or Char:FindFirstChild("ForceField")) do task.wait(0.2) end
				SpawnBuyUntil = 0
				SpawnBuyingDone = true
			end)
		end
	end
	if not Config.AntiStompTeleportBack or not Config.AntiStompPos then
		return
	end
	local BackRoot = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
	if not BackRoot then
		return
	end
	for _ = 1, 3 do
		BackRoot.CFrame = Config.AntiStompPos
		RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
	end
end)

RunService:BindToRenderStep("FlameState", 100, function()
	getgenv().FlameAttacking = Following or Stomping or InVoid or BuyBusy
	local ReturnCF = VoidReturn
	if InVoid and ReturnCF and math.abs(ReturnCF.Position.X) < 100000 and math.abs(ReturnCF.Position.Z) < 100000 then
		getgenv().FlameServerCFrame = ReturnCF
	else
		getgenv().FlameServerCFrame = nil
	end
	local Viewport = Workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize
	local Point = Vector2.new(Viewport.X / 2, Viewport.Y / 2 + 46)
	StateShadow.Position = Point + Vector2.new(1, 1)
	StateText.Position = Point
	local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
	local State
	if Character then
		if IsReloading() then
			State = "reloading"
		elseif Following then
			State = "following"
		elseif Stomping then
			State = "stomping"
		elseif BuyBusy then
			State = "purchasing"
		elseif InVoid then
			State = "void"
		elseif not LocalReady(Character) then
			if Character:FindFirstChild("FORCEFIELD") or Character:FindFirstChild("ForceField") then
				State = "invulnerable"
			else
				State = "unsafe"
			end
		end
	end
	StateText.Text = State or ""
	StateShadow.Text = State or ""
	StateText.Visible = State ~= nil
	StateShadow.Visible = State ~= nil
end);
end)();


	(function()
		local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")

		if getgenv().FlameMoveLoop then
			getgenv().FlameMoveLoop:Disconnect()
			getgenv().FlameMoveLoop = nil
		end

		if getgenv().FlameJumpLoop then
			getgenv().FlameJumpLoop:Disconnect()
			getgenv().FlameJumpLoop = nil
		end

		local function Typing()
			local Ok, Focused = pcall(function()
				return UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox()
			end)
			return Ok and Focused ~= nil
		end

		local function BotBusy()
			return Config.Enabled and (Following or Stomping or InVoid or BuyBusy)
		end

		getgenv().FlameJumpLoop = UserInputService.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
			if not Config.InfJump then
				return
			end
			local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
			local Humanoid = Character and Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if Humanoid and Humanoid.Health > 0 then
				Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
			end
		end)

		getgenv().FlameMoveLoop = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(Delta)
			local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
			local Humanoid = Character and Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			local Root = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not Humanoid or not Root then
				return
			end

			if Config.WalkSpeed and Humanoid.WalkSpeed ~= Config.WalkSpeedValue then
				Humanoid.WalkSpeed = Config.WalkSpeedValue
			end

			if Config.JumpPower then
				if Humanoid.UseJumpPower ~= true then
					Humanoid.UseJumpPower = true
				end
				if Humanoid.JumpPower ~= Config.JumpPowerValue then
					Humanoid.JumpPower = Config.JumpPowerValue
				end
			end

			if BotBusy() then
				return
			end

			if Config.CFrameSpeed then
				Root.CFrame = Root.CFrame + Humanoid.MoveDirection * Config.CFrameSpeedAmount
			end

			if Config.CFrameFly then
				Root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				Root.Velocity = Vector3.zero
				local Direction = Vector3.zero
				local View = Workspace.CurrentCamera
				if View and not Typing() then
					if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then
						Direction = Direction + View.CFrame.LookVector
					end
					if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then
						Direction = Direction - View.CFrame.LookVector
					end
					if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then
						Direction = Direction + View.CFrame.RightVector
					end
					if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then
						Direction = Direction - View.CFrame.RightVector
					end
					if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then
						Direction = Direction + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
					end
					if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) then
						Direction = Direction - Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
					end
				end
				if Direction.Magnitude > 0 then
					Root.CFrame = Root.CFrame + Direction.Unit * Config.CFrameFlySpeed * Delta
				end
			end
		end)
	end)()
